================ FILE: src/lib/syncResilience.ts ================
import { disableNetwork, enableNetwork } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db } from './firebase';
import { localDb } from './localDb';
import { processPendingSync, getConnectionStatus } from './syncQueue';

/**
 * Sync resilience helpers (additive module).
 *
 * 1. initNetworkRecovery: restarts the Firestore network layer after the app was in background.
 *    iOS Safari / PWA suspends WebChannel connections; the listener can stay "connected" but silently stop
 *    receiving updates. disableNetwork -> enableNetwork forces a clean reconnect (pending writes are preserved).
 * 2. resyncIfStale: delta documents are purged after 7 days. A device that has not synced for longer
 *    than that can never catch up through deltas, so wipe the local cache and reload once.
 */

const HIDDEN_THRESHOLD_MS = 45 * 1000;
const WALL_CLOCK_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 15 * 1000;
const WALL_CLOCK_JUMP_THRESHOLD_MS = 45 * 1000;
const IDLE_FOCUS_THRESHOLD_MS = 3 * 60 * 1000;
const STALE_RESYNC_MS = 6 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // < 7-day delta retention
const RESYNC_GUARD_KEY = 'taka_full_resync_guard_v1';

let activeRecoveryPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;

/**
 * If Firestore network is currently cycling (disableNetwork -> enableNetwork),
 * wait for it to finish before issuing getDocsFromServer queries.
 */
export async function waitForNetworkRecovery(): Promise<void> {
  if (activeRecoveryPromise) {
    try {
      await activeRecoveryPromise;
    } catch {}
  }
}

export function initNetworkRecovery(onRecovered?: () => void): () => void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof document === 'undefined') return () => {};

  let hiddenAt = 0;
  let lastActiveAt = Date.now();
  let lastWallClockTick = Date.now();
  let lastRecoveredAt = 0;
  let busy = false;

  const recover = async () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (busy || now - lastRecoveredAt < 5000) return;
    busy = true;
    lastRecoveredAt = now;
    const p = (async () => {
      try {
        await disableNetwork(db);
        await enableNetwork(db);
        await processPendingSync();
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[syncResilience] Network recovery failed:', err);
      } finally {
        busy = false;
        activeRecoveryPromise = null;
        lastActiveAt = Date.now();
        lastWallClockTick = Date.now();
        try { onRecovered?.(); } catch {}
      }
    })();
    activeRecoveryPromise = p;
    return p;
  };

  const handleVisibility = () => {
    if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
      hiddenAt = Date.now();
      return;
    }
    const now = Date.now();
    const hiddenDuration = hiddenAt ? now - hiddenAt : 0;
    const idleDuration = now - lastActiveAt;
    hiddenAt = 0;
    lastActiveAt = now;
    if (hiddenDuration > HIDDEN_THRESHOLD_MS || idleDuration > IDLE_FOCUS_THRESHOLD_MS) {
      recover();
    }
  };

  // Page restored from the iOS back/forward cache: sockets are certainly dead.
  const handlePageShow = (e: Event) => {
    if ((e as PageTransitionEvent).persisted) recover();
  };

  // Network came back (e.g. iPhone/Desktop was offline): force a clean reconnect so queued offline writes flush now
  const handleOnline = () => { recover(); };

  // Desktop overnight wake: tab may have stayed "visible" all night while monitor/OS slept or sat idle
  const handleFocusOrSleepWake = () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastActiveAt > IDLE_FOCUS_THRESHOLD_MS) {
      lastActiveAt = now;
      recover();
    }
  };

  const handleUserActivity = () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastActiveAt > IDLE_FOCUS_THRESHOLD_MS) {
      lastActiveAt = now;
      recover();
    } else {
      lastActiveAt = now;
    }
  };

  // Wall-clock sleep detector: catches OS sleep/wake even when tab stayed visible and focused
  const wallClockTimer = setInterval(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const elapsed = now - lastWallClockTick;
    lastWallClockTick = now;
    if (elapsed > WALL_CLOCK_JUMP_THRESHOLD_MS && document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
      recover();
    }
  }, WALL_CLOCK_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibility);
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', handlePageShow);
  window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
  window.addEventListener('focus', handleFocusOrSleepWake);
  window.addEventListener('taka_sleep_wake_recovery', recover);
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown', handleUserActivity, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleUserActivity, { passive: true });

  return () => {
    clearInterval(wallClockTimer);
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibility);
    window.removeEventListener('pageshow', handlePageShow);
    window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.removeEventListener('focus', handleFocusOrSleepWake);
    window.removeEventListener('taka_sleep_wake_recovery', recover);
    window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handleUserActivity);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleUserActivity);
  };
}

/**
 * If lastSyncTime is older than the delta retention window, clear local caches and reload once
 * so every listener re-reads fresh data from Firestore. Returns true when a resync was triggered.
 */
export async function resyncIfStale(lastSyncTime: number, lastSyncKey: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  if (!lastSyncTime || Date.now() - lastSyncTime < STALE_RESYNC_MS) return false;

  // Loop guard: never trigger twice within 10 minutes.
  try {
    const last = Number(window.sessionStorage.getItem(RESYNC_GUARD_KEY) || 0);
    if (last && Date.now() - last < 10 * 60 * 1000) return false;
    window.sessionStorage.setItem(RESYNC_GUARD_KEY, String(Date.now()));
  } catch {}

  console.warn('[syncResilience] Local data older than delta retention. Running full resync.');
  try {
    await Promise.all([
      localDb.projects.clear(),
      localDb.tasks.clear(),
      localDb.costs.clear(),
      localDb.delegations.clear(),
      localDb.syncMeta.clear(),
    ]);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[syncResilience] Failed to clear Dexie:', err);
  }

  try {
    const keys: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < window.localStorage.length; i++) {
      const k = window.localStorage.key(i);
      if (k && (k.startsWith('taka_year_data_v1_') || k === 'taka_years_meta_fingerprint_v1')) keys.push(k);
    }
    keys.forEach((k) => window.localStorage.removeItem(k));
    window.localStorage.setItem(lastSyncKey, String(Date.now() - 30 * 60 * 1000));
  } catch {}

  window.location.reload();
  return true;
}

export const connectionMonitor = {
  onOnline: (callback: () => void) => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
    const handler = () => {
      processPendingSync().catch(() => {});
      callback();
    };
    window.addEventListener('online', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('online', handler);
  },

  onOffline: (callback: () => void) => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
    window.addEventListener('offline', callback);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('offline', callback);
  },

  destroy: () => {
    // Cleanup monitor handlers
  },
};

export const listenerManager = {
  getStatus: () => {
    const isOnline = getConnectionStatus() === 'online';
    return {
      totalListeners: 4,
      activeListeners: isOnline ? 4 : 0,
      listeners: [
        { id: 'projects-listener', isActive: isOnline, collection: 'taka_projects' },
        { id: 'tasks-listener', isActive: isOnline, collection: 'taka_tasks' },
        { id: 'costs-listener', isActive: isOnline, collection: 'taka_costs' },
        { id: 'delegations-listener', isActive: isOnline, collection: 'taka_delegations' },
      ],
      isOnline,
    };
  },
};

================ FILE: src/lib/deltaPullService.ts ================
import {
  collection,
  query,
  where,
  orderBy,
  limit,
  getDocsFromServer,
  Timestamp,
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db, APP_ID } from './firebase';
import { TakaDelta } from '../types';
import { resyncIfStale, waitForNetworkRecovery } from './syncResilience';
import { 
  deltaTime, 
  getLastDeltaSyncTime, 
  getRestoreCutoffTimestamp, 
  appliedDeltaIds,
} from './deltaSyncService';
import { DELTA_COLLECTION, CLOCK_SKEW_OVERLAP_MS, MAX_APPLIED_IDS, LAST_SYNC_KEY } from './deltaConstants';
import { applyIncomingDeltas } from './deltaApplyService';
import { safeSetItemLocal } from './storage';

let inFlightPullPromise: Promise<number> | null = null;
let lastPullCompletedAt = 0;
let pendingForcedFollowUp = false;

/**
 * Kéo các delta thay đổi từ server về client kể từ lastSyncTime và áp dụng vào Zustand
 * Tiết kiệm hơn 80% Read so với việc onSnapshot cả collection
 * B4: In-flight deduplication prevents concurrent identical queries across tabs/effects
 */
export async function pullAndApplyDeltas(yearFilter?: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<number> {
  if (inFlightPullPromise) {
    if (force) pendingForcedFollowUp = true;
    return inFlightPullPromise;
  }
  const now = Date.now();
  if (!force && now - lastPullCompletedAt < 2000) {
    return 0;
  }

  let pullSucceeded = false;
  inFlightPullPromise = (async () => {
    await waitForNetworkRecovery();

    const lastSyncTime = getLastDeltaSyncTime();
    const restoreCutoff = getRestoreCutoffTimestamp();

    // Device offline longer than delta retention -> deltas are gone, do a full resync.
    if (await resyncIfStale(lastSyncTime, LAST_SYNC_KEY)) return 0;

    const colRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', DELTA_COLLECTION);

    try {
      const deltas: TakaDelta[] = [];
      let currentSince = Math.max(lastSyncTime - CLOCK_SKEW_OVERLAP_MS, restoreCutoff);
      let iteration = 0;
      const MAX_PAGES = 5;

      while (iteration < MAX_PAGES) {
        iteration++;
        await waitForNetworkRecovery();
        const q = query(
          colRef,
          where('serverTime', '>', Timestamp.fromMillis(currentSince)),
          orderBy('serverTime', 'asc'),
          limit(500)
        );

        let snapshot: any;
        try {
          snapshot = await getDocsFromServer(q);
        } catch (serverErr) {
          // Nếu mạng đang reconnect lúc iOS/Desktop mới thức dậy, KHÔNG đánh dấu pullSucceeded để lần gọi ngay sau đó chạy được ngay
          console.warn('[deltaSync] getDocsFromServer not reachable yet (device offline or reconnecting):', serverErr);
          return 0;
        }

        if (!snapshot || snapshot.empty) {
          break;
        }

        let pageMaxTime = currentSince;
        snapshot.forEach((d: any) => {
          const item = d.data() as TakaDelta;
          const itemId = item.id || d.id;
          if (appliedDeltaIds.has(itemId)) {
            if (deltaTime(item) > pageMaxTime) pageMaxTime = deltaTime(item);
            return;
          }
          appliedDeltaIds.add(itemId);
          if (appliedDeltaIds.size > MAX_APPLIED_IDS) {
            const oldest = appliedDeltaIds.values().next().value;
            if (oldest !== undefined) appliedDeltaIds.delete(oldest);
          }
          deltas.push(item);
          if (deltaTime(item) > pageMaxTime) {
            pageMaxTime = deltaTime(item);
          }
        });

        currentSince = pageMaxTime;
        if (snapshot.size < 500) {
          break;
        }
      }

      pullSucceeded = true;

      if (deltas.length === 0) {
        // Server confirmed 0 new deltas: advance sync cursor safely
        const safeRecentTime = Math.max(lastSyncTime, Date.now() - 60000);
        safeSetItemLocal(LAST_SYNC_KEY, String(safeRecentTime));
        return 0;
      }

      return applyIncomingDeltas(deltas);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[deltaSync] Error pulling deltas:', err);
      return 0;
    } finally {
      if (pullSucceeded) {
        lastPullCompletedAt = Date.now();
      }
      inFlightPullPromise = null;
      if (pendingForcedFollowUp) {
        pendingForcedFollowUp = false;
        setTimeout(() => {
          pullAndApplyDeltas(yearFilter, true).catch(() => {});
        }, 150);
      }
    }
  })();

  return inFlightPullPromise;
}

================ FILE: src/lib/deltaOrchestratorService.ts ================
import { useAppStore } from '../store/useAppStore';
import { pullAndApplyDeltas } from './deltaPullService';
import { initNetworkRecovery } from './syncResilience';
import { startDeltaRealtimeListener } from './deltaRealtimeListener';
import { purgeOldDeltas } from './deltaPurgeService';

let syncIntervalTimer: any = null;
let isHibernating = false;
let lastActivityTime = Date.now();
const IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes inactivity threshold

/**
 * Tự động Dọn dẹp Delta cũ (Delta Retention TTL & Pruning)
 */
export async function pruneExpiredDeltas(retentionDays: number = 7): Promise<number> {
  return purgeOldDeltas(retentionDays * 24);
}

/**
 * Khởi động Background Delta Sync Poller với Adaptive Tab Hibernation & Inactivity Sleep
 * - Khi ẩn tab hoặc người dùng không hoạt động > 5 phút: Tạm dừng định thời hoàn toàn (0 Idle Read).
 * - Khi quay lại tab hoặc tương tác: Lập tức sync tức thì (0ms) và kích hoạt lại interval.
 */
export function startDeltaSyncService(intervalMs: number = 30 * 60 * 1000): () => void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};

  const stopRealtime = startDeltaRealtimeListener();

  let lastTriggerAt = 0;
  const triggerSync = (force: boolean = false) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!force && now - lastTriggerAt < 10000) return;
    lastTriggerAt = now;
    const year = useAppStore.getState().globalYearFilter;
    pullAndApplyDeltas(year, force).catch(() => {});
  };

  const stopPolling = () => {
    if (syncIntervalTimer) {
      clearInterval(syncIntervalTimer);
      syncIntervalTimer = null;
    }
  };

  const startPolling = () => {
    stopPolling();
    syncIntervalTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
        hibernate();
        return;
      }
      if (Date.now() - lastActivityTime > IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        hibernate();
        return;
      }
      triggerSync(false);
    }, intervalMs);
  };

  const hibernate = () => {
    if (isHibernating) return;
    isHibernating = true;
    stopPolling();
  };

  const wakeAndSync = () => {
    lastActivityTime = Date.now();
    isHibernating = false;
    triggerSync(true); // Ép đồng bộ ngay khi thức giấc (bỏ qua throttle)
    startPolling();
  };

  // 1. Initial boot sync & start timer
  triggerSync(true);
  startPolling();
  // Khi syncResilience khôi phục xong mạng (sau disableNetwork -> enableNetwork), ép pull delta ngay lập tức với force=true!
  const stopNetworkRecovery = initNetworkRecovery(() => {
    triggerSync(true);
    // Phát sự kiện để deltaRealtimeListener gắn lại (re-attach) trên socket mới
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('taka_network_recovered'));
  });

  // 2. Tab Visibility Listener (Page Visibility API - Hibernation)
  let hiddenSince = 0;
  const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
      const hiddenDuration = hiddenSince ? Date.now() - hiddenSince : 0;
      hiddenSince = 0;
      lastActivityTime = Date.now();
      if (isHibernating || hiddenDuration >= 15000) {
        wakeAndSync();
      } else {
        startPolling();
      }
    } else {
      hiddenSince = Date.now();
      hibernate();
    }
  };

  // 3. Online network reconnection, Window Focus & User Activity (Desktop overnight idle / iPad Split-View)
  const handleOnline = () => {
    wakeAndSync();
  };
  const handleFocus = () => {
    lastActivityTime = Date.now();
    // Force a server pull on focus if the poller had entered hibernation (>5 min idle)
    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && isHibernating) {
      wakeAndSync();
    }
  };
  const handleUserInteraction = () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const wasIdleLong = now - lastActivityTime > IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS;
    lastActivityTime = now;
    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && (isHibernating || wasIdleLong)) {
      wakeAndSync();
    }
  };

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
  window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
  window.addEventListener('focus', handleFocus);
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown', handleUserInteraction, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleUserInteraction, { passive: true });

  return () => {
    stopPolling();
    stopNetworkRecovery();
    stopRealtime();
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
    window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.removeEventListener('focus', handleFocus);
    window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handleUserInteraction);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleUserInteraction);
  };
}

export async function triggerImmediateDeltaSync(year?: string): Promise<number> {
  lastActivityTime = Date.now();
  isHibernating = false;
  return pullAndApplyDeltas(year || useAppStore.getState().globalYearFilter, true);
}
================ END FILE: src/lib/deltaOrchestratorService.ts ================

================ FILE: src/lib/deltaRealtimeListener.ts ================
import { collection, onSnapshot, orderBy, query, where, Timestamp } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { onAuthStateChanged } from 'firebase/auth';
import { auth, db, APP_ID } from './firebase';
import { applyIncomingDeltas, filterUnappliedDeltas, getLastDeltaSyncTime, deltaTime, pullAndApplyDeltas } from './deltaSyncService';
import type { TakaDelta } from '../types';

/**
 * Real-time delta channel.
 *
 * One onSnapshot listener on taka_deltas replaces 25s polling:
 *  - idle cost = 0 reads (previously >= 1 read per poll, ~1100+/user/day)
 *  - changes arrive in ~1s instead of 25s - 3 min
 *  - billed once per delta doc; cached deltas (persistent cache) are free
 * The listener is detached when the tab has been hidden > 2 min and re-attached (since = lastSyncTime) on return.
 */

const OVERLAP_MS = 10 * 1000; // serverTime overlap window, duplicates filtered by delta id
const DETACH_AFTER_HIDDEN_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;
const RETRY_MS = 30 * 1000;

export function startDeltaRealtimeListener(): () => void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};

  let unsub: (() => void) | null = null;
  let buffer: TakaDelta[] = [];
  let flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  let detachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  let signedIn = !!auth.currentUser;
  let stopped = false;

  const flush = () => {
    flushTimer = null;
    const batch = filterUnappliedDeltas(buffer);
    buffer = [];
    if (batch.length === 0) return;
    batch.sort((a, b) => deltaTime(a) - deltaTime(b));
    try {
      applyIncomingDeltas(batch);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[deltaRealtime] apply failed:', err);
    }
  };

  const detach = () => {
    if (unsub) {
      try { unsub(); } catch {}
      unsub = null;
    }
  };

  const attach = () => {
    if (stopped || unsub || !signedIn) return;
    const since = getLastDeltaSyncTime() - OVERLAP_MS;
    const q = query(
      collection(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_deltas'),
      where('serverTime', '>', Timestamp.fromMillis(since)),
      orderBy('serverTime', 'asc')
    );
    unsub = onSnapshot(
      q,
      (snap) => {
        snap.docChanges().forEach((c) => {
          if (c.type === 'added' || c.type === 'modified') {
            const raw = c.doc.data({ serverTimestamps: 'estimate' }) as TakaDelta;
            buffer.push({ ...raw, id: raw.id || c.doc.id });
          }
        });
        if (buffer.length > 0 && !flushTimer) flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, 20);
      },
      (err) => {
        console.warn('[deltaRealtime] listener error, retrying:', err);
        detach();
        if (!stopped && !retryTimer) {
          retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            retryTimer = null;
            attach();
          }, RETRY_MS);
        }
      }
    );
  };

  if (signedIn) attach();

  let hiddenAt = 0;
  let lastActiveAt = Date.now();
  const IDLE_REATTACH_MS = 3 * 60 * 1000;

  const onVisibility = () => {
    if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
      hiddenAt = Date.now();
      if (detachTimer) clearTimeout(detachTimer);
      detachTimer = setTimeout(detach, DETACH_AFTER_HIDDEN_MS);
    } else {
      if (detachTimer) { clearTimeout(detachTimer); detachTimer = null; }
      const now = Date.now();
      const hiddenFor = hiddenAt ? now - hiddenAt : Infinity;
      hiddenAt = 0;
      lastActiveAt = now;
      // Ẩn ngắn (dưới 15 giây, ví dụ chuyển tab): kết nối vẫn sống và listener vẫn nhận delta realtime,
      // không cần tạo lại listener hay kéo delta (tránh đọc lại cửa sổ 3 phút).
      if (unsub && hiddenFor < 15000) return;
      // Ẩn lâu: ép tạo lại socket để phục hồi kết nối WebChannel bị iOS/Safari/Desktop treo
      detach();
      attach();
      pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  const onNetworkRecovered = () => {
    lastActiveAt = Date.now();
    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
      detach();
      attach();
      pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  const handlePageShow = (e: PageTransitionEvent) => {
    if (e.persisted) {
      lastActiveAt = Date.now();
      detach();
      attach();
      pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  const handleOnline = () => {
    lastActiveAt = Date.now();
    detach();
    attach();
    pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true).catch(() => {});
  };

  const handleFocus = () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const idleFor = now - lastActiveAt;
    lastActiveAt = now;
    // Nếu Desktop để qua đêm (tab vẫn visible nhưng không tương tác > 3 phút), socket có thể bị treo: ép gắn lại
    if (idleFor > IDLE_REATTACH_MS || !unsub) {
      detach();
      attach();
      pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  const handleActivity = () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastActiveAt > IDLE_REATTACH_MS) {
      lastActiveAt = now;
      detach();
      attach();
      pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true).catch(() => {});
    } else {
      lastActiveAt = now;
    }
  };

  const unsubAuth = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
    signedIn = !!user;
    if (user) attach();
    else detach();
  });

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
  window.addEventListener('taka_network_recovered', onNetworkRecovered);
  window.addEventListener('taka_sleep_wake_recovery', onNetworkRecovered);
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', handlePageShow as any);
  window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
  window.addEventListener('focus', handleFocus);
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown', handleActivity, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleActivity, { passive: true });

  return () => {
    stopped = true;
    unsubAuth();
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    window.removeEventListener('taka_network_recovered', onNetworkRecovered);
    window.removeEventListener('taka_sleep_wake_recovery', onNetworkRecovered);
    window.removeEventListener('pageshow', handlePageShow as any);
    window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.removeEventListener('focus', handleFocus);
    window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handleActivity);
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleActivity);
    if (flushTimer) clearTimeout(flushTimer);
    if (detachTimer) clearTimeout(detachTimer);
    if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
    detach();
  };
}

================ FILE: src/lib/deltaApplyService.ts ================
import { useAppStore } from '../store/useAppStore';
import { usePerformanceStore } from '../store/usePerformanceStore';
import { TakaDelta, TakaDeltaItem, Project, Task, Cost, DelegationGroup } from '../types';
import { localDb } from './localDb';
import { applyDeltasToLocalDatabase } from './backgroundSyncWorker';
import { triggerBatchChangeFlash } from './changeFlashService';
import { broadcastAppliedDeltas } from './broadcastSyncService';
import { applyFieldPatch } from './deltaPatchService';
import { getTasksForProject, calculateProjectComputedDates } from './projectDatesService';
import { resolveInvalidations } from './deltaInvalidateService';
import { 
  deltaTime, 
  getLastDeltaSyncTime, 
  getRestoreCutoffTimestamp, 
  markRestoreVersionCompleted
} from './deltaSyncService';
import { CLIENT_SESSION_ID, DELTA_COLLECTION, LAST_SYNC_KEY } from './deltaConstants';
import { safeSetItemLocal } from './storage';
import { markTasksDeleted } from './taskDeletedTracker';

/**
 * Applies a list of (already de-duplicated) deltas to Zustand + Dexie. Shared by the real-time listener and the
 * safety poll. Returns the number of deltas processed.
 */
export function applyIncomingDeltas(deltas: TakaDelta[]): number {
  if (!deltas || deltas.length === 0) return 0;

  // Ghi nhận vào Performance Store để giám sát
  usePerformanceStore
    .getState()
    .trackGetDoc(DELTA_COLLECTION, false, deltas.length, `Pulled ${deltas.length} deltas`);

  const lastSyncTime = getLastDeltaSyncTime();
  let maxTime = lastSyncTime;
  const store = useAppStore.getState();

  // Map chứa các thay đổi gộp để cập nhật nguyên tử vào Zustand
  let updatedProjects = [...(store.projects || [])];
  let updatedTasks = [...(store.tasks || [])];
  let updatedCosts = [...(store.costs || [])];
  let updatedDelegations = [...(store.delegationGroups || [])];

  let projectsChanged = false;
  let tasksChanged = false;
  let costsChanged = false;
  let delegationsChanged = false;

  const invalidations: TakaDeltaItem[] = [];

  // Các dự án có task thay đổi trong lô delta này (để chỉ tính lại đúng các dự án đó)
  const touchedProjectIds = new Set<string>();
  const touchedProjectCodes = new Set<string>();
  const noteTouchedProject = (t: any) => {
    if (!t) return;
    if (t.projectId) touchedProjectIds.add(String(t.projectId));
    if (t.projectCode) touchedProjectCodes.add(String(t.projectCode).trim().toLowerCase());
  };

  const applyItem = (
    docId: string,
    collectionNameRaw: string,
    action: 'create' | 'update' | 'delete',
    data: any,
    year?: string
  ) => {
    // Một số nơi ghi delta với tên đường dẫn thật `taka_delegation_groups`
    const collectionName =
      collectionNameRaw === 'taka_delegation_groups' ? 'taka_delegations' : collectionNameRaw;
    if (
      collectionName === 'taka_delegations' &&
      !store.isDelegationsRequested &&
      store.delegationGroups.length === 0
    ) {
      return;
    }

    // [Safety Guard] Prevent old deltas (e.g. synthetic or out-of-order) from clobbering newer state
    const tsOf = (v: any): number => {
      if (!v) return 0;
      if (typeof v === 'string') {
        const n = Date.parse(v);
        return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
      }
      if (typeof v?.toMillis === 'function') {
        const n = v.toMillis();
        return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
      }
      if (typeof v?.seconds === 'number') {
        return v.seconds * 1000 + Math.floor((v.nanoseconds || 0) / 1e6);
      }
      const n = Number(v);
      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
    };

    const isDelete = action === 'delete' || data?.isDeleted === true;

    // Chỉ bỏ qua khi delta CÓ updatedAt hợp lệ và CŨ HƠN bản đang giữ.
    // Delta xóa, delta không có data, và delta vá không mang updatedAt luôn được áp dụng
    // (thứ tự đã do serverTime quyết định). Tuyệt đối không đọc data.updatedAt khi data có thể null.
    const isStaleVsLocal = (existing: any): boolean => {
      if (isDelete || !data) return false;
      const incoming = tsOf(data.updatedAt);
      if (!incoming) return false;
      return tsOf(existing?.updatedAt) > incoming;
    };

    switch (collectionName) {
      case 'taka_projects': {
        const idx = updatedProjects.findIndex((p) => p.id === docId);
        if (isDelete || data?.isDeleted) {
          if (idx !== -1) {
            projectsChanged = true;
            updatedProjects = updatedProjects.filter((p) => p.id !== docId);
          }
          break;
        }
        if (idx !== -1 && isStaleVsLocal(updatedProjects[idx])) return;

        projectsChanged = true;
        let nextProj: Project | null = null;
        if (idx !== -1) {
          const prevProj = updatedProjects[idx];
          nextProj = applyFieldPatch(prevProj, data) as Project;
          if (Array.isArray(data?.taskSummaries)) {
            nextProj.taskCount = data.taskSummaries.length;
          }
          updatedProjects[idx] = nextProj;
        } else if (action === 'create') {
          // Ghost record guard: only add if action is 'create', never resurrect from an 'update' patch
          nextProj = { id: docId, ...data } as Project;
          updatedProjects.push(nextProj);
        }

        // Reconcile local tasks against authoritative incoming project.taskSummaries
        if (nextProj && Array.isArray(data?.taskSummaries)) {
          const hasReliableIds =
            data.taskSummaries.length === 0 ||
            data.taskSummaries.every((s: any) => Boolean(s?.id));
          if (hasReliableIds) {
            const validIdsLower = new Set(
              data.taskSummaries.map((s: any) => String(s.id).trim().toLowerCase())
            );
            const nowMs = Date.now();
            const pIdStr = String(nextProj.id);
            const pCodeLower = String(nextProj.code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const pStoreLower = String(nextProj.store || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const pYearStr = nextProj.year !== undefined && nextProj.year !== null ? String(nextProj.year).trim() : '';
            const rawCodes = (nextProj as any).linkCodes
              ? `${nextProj.code || ''}, ${(nextProj as any).linkCodes}`
              : String(nextProj.code || '');
            const pCodeParts = rawCodes.toLowerCase().split(/[,;/]+/).map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

            const zombieIds: string[] = [];
            updatedTasks = updatedTasks.filter((t) => {
              if (!t || !t.id || t.id.startsWith('ts_')) return true;
              const matchesProj =
                (t.projectId && String(t.projectId) === pIdStr) ||
                (!t.projectId &&
                  t.projectCode &&
                  (String(t.projectCode).trim().toLowerCase() === pCodeLower ||
                    pCodeParts.includes(String(t.projectCode).trim().toLowerCase())) &&
                  (!pStoreLower || !t.projectStore || String(t.projectStore).trim().toLowerCase() === pStoreLower) &&
                  (!pYearStr || !t.projectYear || String(t.projectYear).trim() === pYearStr));
              if (!matchesProj) return true;
              if (validIdsLower.has(String(t.id).trim().toLowerCase())) return true;
              const updatedMs = tsOf(t.updatedAt);
              if (updatedMs && nowMs - updatedMs <= 30000) return true;
              zombieIds.push(t.id);
              return false;
            });
            if (zombieIds.length > 0) {
              tasksChanged = true;
              markTasksDeleted(zombieIds, pIdStr);
              zombieIds.forEach((zId) => {
                localDb.tasks.delete(zId).catch(() => {});
              });
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'taka_tasks': {
        const idx = updatedTasks.findIndex((t) => t.id === docId);
        const existingTask = idx !== -1 ? updatedTasks[idx] : null;
        noteTouchedProject(existingTask);
        noteTouchedProject(data);
        if (isDelete || data?.isDeleted) {
          const pIdHint = data?.projectId || existingTask?.projectId;
          markTasksDeleted([docId], pIdHint ? String(pIdHint) : undefined);
          localDb.tasks.delete(docId).catch(() => {});
          if (idx !== -1) {
            tasksChanged = true;
            updatedTasks = updatedTasks.filter((t) => t.id !== docId);
          }
          const docIdLower = String(docId).trim().toLowerCase();
          const delNameLower = String(data?.name || existingTask?.name || '').trim().toLowerCase();
          const delCodeLower = String(data?.taskCodePhu || existingTask?.taskCodePhu || '').trim().toLowerCase();

          // Cắt đứt ngay lập tức task đã xóa khỏi project.taskSummaries trên máy nhận (chống hiện tượng bóng ma hồi sinh task trên iPad)
          updatedProjects = updatedProjects.map((p) => {
            if (Array.isArray(p.taskSummaries) && p.taskSummaries.length > 0) {
              const filteredSummaries = p.taskSummaries.filter((s: any) => {
                const sId = s?.id ? String(s.id).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
                if (sId && sId === docIdLower) return false;
                if (!sId && delNameLower && String(s?.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === delNameLower) {
                  const sCode = s?.taskCodePhu ? String(s.taskCodePhu).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
                  if (!delCodeLower || !sCode || sCode === delCodeLower) return false;
                }
                return true;
              });
              if (filteredSummaries.length !== p.taskSummaries.length) {
                projectsChanged = true;
                markTasksDeleted([docId], p.id ? String(p.id) : undefined);
                noteTouchedProject({ projectId: p.id, projectCode: p.code });
                return {
                  ...p,
                  taskSummaries: filteredSummaries,
                  taskCount: filteredSummaries.length,
                };
              }
            }
            return p;
          });
          break;
        }
        if (idx !== -1 && isStaleVsLocal(updatedTasks[idx])) return;

        const mergedTask = (idx !== -1 ? applyFieldPatch(updatedTasks[idx], data) : { id: docId, ...data }) as Task;

        // Upsert task summary in matching project if project already has taskSummaries (keeps collapsed/General view 100% synced)
        if (mergedTask.start && mergedTask.finish && mergedTask.name) {
          const docIdLower = String(docId).trim().toLowerCase();
          const tProjId = mergedTask.projectId ? String(mergedTask.projectId) : '';
          const tCodeLower = mergedTask.projectCode ? String(mergedTask.projectCode).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
          const tStoreLower = mergedTask.projectStore ? String(mergedTask.projectStore).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
          const tYearStr = mergedTask.projectYear !== undefined && mergedTask.projectYear !== null ? String(mergedTask.projectYear).trim() : '';

          updatedProjects = updatedProjects.map((p) => {
            const rawCodes = (p as any).linkCodes ? `${p.code || ''}, ${(p as any).linkCodes}` : String(p.code || '');
            const pCodeParts = rawCodes.toLowerCase().split(/[,;/]+/).map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const matchesProj =
              (tProjId && String(p.id) === tProjId) ||
              (!tProjId &&
                tCodeLower &&
                (String(p.code || '').trim().toLowerCase() === tCodeLower || pCodeParts.includes(tCodeLower)) &&
                (!tStoreLower || !p.store || String(p.store).trim().toLowerCase() === tStoreLower) &&
                (!tYearStr || !p.year || String(p.year).trim() === tYearStr));
            if (!matchesProj) return p;

            const currentSummaries = Array.isArray(p.taskSummaries) ? [...p.taskSummaries] : [];
            const sIdx = currentSummaries.findIndex((s: any) => String(s?.id || '').trim().toLowerCase() === docIdLower);
            const summaryItem = {
              ...(sIdx !== -1 ? currentSummaries[sIdx] : {}),
              id: docId,
              name: mergedTask.name,
              start: mergedTask.start,
              finish: mergedTask.finish,
              orderIndex: mergedTask.orderIndex ?? (sIdx !== -1 ? currentSummaries[sIdx].orderIndex : currentSummaries.length),
              ...(mergedTask.baselineStart ? { baselineStart: mergedTask.baselineStart } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.baselineFinish ? { baselineFinish: mergedTask.baselineFinish } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.approval ? { approval: mergedTask.approval } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.party ? { party: mergedTask.party } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.taskCodePhu ? { taskCodePhu: mergedTask.taskCodePhu } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.parentTaskId ? { parentTaskId: mergedTask.parentTaskId } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.duration !== undefined ? { duration: mergedTask.duration } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.runningStatus ? { runningStatus: mergedTask.runningStatus } : {}),
              ...(mergedTask.delegation !== undefined ? { delegation: mergedTask.delegation } : {}),
            };
            if (sIdx !== -1) {
              currentSummaries[sIdx] = summaryItem;
              projectsChanged = true;
              return { ...p, taskSummaries: currentSummaries, taskCount: currentSummaries.length };
            } else if (action === 'create') {
              currentSummaries.push(summaryItem);
              projectsChanged = true;
              return { ...p, taskSummaries: currentSummaries, taskCount: currentSummaries.length };
            }
            return p;
          });
        }

        if (idx !== -1) {
          tasksChanged = true;
          updatedTasks[idx] = mergedTask;
        } else if (action === 'create') {
          // Tri-Gate & Partial-Hydration Guard (Rule 14):
          // Only push new task into in-memory Zustand if Tasks slice is requested AND the project is hydrated/expanded
          // (Dexie still receives 100% of deltas via applyDeltasToLocalDatabase below)
          if (store.isTasksRequested || updatedTasks.length > 0) {
            const expandedSet = new Set([...(store.expandedProjectIds || []), ...(store.searchActiveProjectIds || [])].map(String));
            const tProjId = mergedTask.projectId ? String(mergedTask.projectId) : '';
            const tCode = mergedTask.projectCode ? String(mergedTask.projectCode) : '';
            const projInMemory = updatedTasks.some(
              (t) =>
                (tProjId && String(t.projectId) === tProjId) ||
                (tCode && String(t.projectCode || '').toLowerCase() === tCode.toLowerCase())
            );
            const matchingProj = updatedProjects.find(
              (p) => (tProjId && String(p.id) === tProjId) || (tCode && String(p.code || '').toLowerCase() === tCode.toLowerCase())
            );
            const isProjExpandedOrEmpty =
              projInMemory ||
              (tProjId && expandedSet.has(tProjId)) ||
              (tCode && expandedSet.has(tCode)) ||
              !matchingProj ||
              (matchingProj.taskSummaries?.length || 0) <= 1;

            if (isProjExpandedOrEmpty) {
              tasksChanged = true;
              updatedTasks.push(mergedTask);
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'taka_costs': {
        const idx = updatedCosts.findIndex((c) => c.id === docId);
        if (isDelete || data?.isDeleted) {
          localDb.costs.delete(docId).catch(() => {});
          if (idx !== -1) {
            costsChanged = true;
            updatedCosts = updatedCosts.filter((c) => c.id !== docId);
          }
          break;
        }
        // Tri-Gate Guard (Rule 14): Do not populate in-memory costs if Cost slice has not been requested yet
        if (!store.isCostsRequested && updatedCosts.length === 0) return;
        if (idx !== -1 && isStaleVsLocal(updatedCosts[idx])) return;

        costsChanged = true;
        if (idx !== -1) {
          updatedCosts[idx] = applyFieldPatch(updatedCosts[idx], data) as Cost;
        } else if (action === 'create') {
          // Ghost record guard: only add if action is 'create', never resurrect from an 'update' patch
          updatedCosts.push({ id: docId, ...data } as Cost);
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'taka_delegations': {
        const idx = updatedDelegations.findIndex((g) => g.id === docId);
        if (isDelete || data?.isDeleted) {
          localDb.delegations.delete(docId).catch(() => {});
          if (idx !== -1) {
            delegationsChanged = true;
            updatedDelegations = updatedDelegations.filter((g) => g.id !== docId);
          }
          break;
        }
        if (idx !== -1 && isStaleVsLocal(updatedDelegations[idx])) return;

        delegationsChanged = true;
        if (idx !== -1) {
          updatedDelegations[idx] = applyFieldPatch(updatedDelegations[idx], data) as DelegationGroup;
        } else if (action === 'create') {
          // Ghost record guard: only add if action is 'create', never resurrect from an 'update' patch
          updatedDelegations.push({ id: docId, ...data } as DelegationGroup);
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  };

  deltas.forEach((delta) => {
    if (deltaTime(delta) > maxTime) {
      maxTime = deltaTime(delta);
    }

    if (delta.docId === 'sys_restore_signal' || delta.data?.type === 'VERSION_RESTORE') {
      const cutoff = delta.data?.restoreCutoff || deltaTime(delta);
      if (cutoff > getRestoreCutoffTimestamp()) {
        markRestoreVersionCompleted(cutoff, true);
        localDb.projects.clear().catch(() => {});
        localDb.tasks.clear().catch(() => {});
        localDb.costs.clear().catch(() => {});
        localDb.delegations.clear().catch(() => {});
      }
      return;
    }

    // 1. Skip self-mutations on this same tab/session to prevent typing jitter (Rule 2)
    if (
      delta.sessionId &&
      delta.sessionId === CLIENT_SESSION_ID
    ) {
      return;
    }

    if (delta.action === 'batch_update' && Array.isArray(delta.batchItems)) {
      delta.batchItems.forEach((item) => {
        if (item.invalidate) {
          invalidations.push(item);
          return;
        }
        try {
          applyItem(item.docId, item.collectionName, item.action, item.data, item.year);
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[deltaSync] applyItem failed (batch item skipped):', item.docId, err);
        }
      });
    } else if (delta.docId && delta.collectionName) {
      try {
        applyItem(delta.docId, delta.collectionName, delta.action as any, delta.data, delta.year);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[deltaSync] applyItem failed (delta skipped):', delta.docId, err);
      }
    }
  });

  // Cập nhật nguyên tử 1 lần duy nhất vào Zustand (0ms UI re-render)
  const touchedProjectsToPersist: Project[] = [];
  if ((tasksChanged || projectsChanged) && (touchedProjectIds.size > 0 || touchedProjectCodes.size > 0)) {
    const delegationGroups = store.delegationGroups || [];
    updatedProjects = updatedProjects.map(p => {
      // Dự án khớp theo id, hoặc theo mã chính / mã liên kết (linkCodes)
      const codeParts = `${p.code || ''},${(p as any).linkCodes || ''}`
        .toLowerCase()
        .split(/[,;/]+/)
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
      const isTouched =
        touchedProjectIds.has(String(p.id)) || codeParts.some((c) => touchedProjectCodes.has(c));
      
      if (!isTouched) return p;

      const pTasks = getTasksForProject(p, updatedTasks);
      const existingSummaries = Array.isArray(p.taskSummaries) ? p.taskSummaries : [];

      // If tasks for this project are not hydrated in RAM (e.g. General tab or collapsed project),
      // compute project dates from p.taskSummaries instead of wiping taskSummaries with an empty pTasks array!
      const useInMemoryTasks = pTasks.length > 0 && pTasks.length >= existingSummaries.length;
      const sourceTasksForCalc: Task[] = useInMemoryTasks
        ? pTasks
        : existingSummaries.map((s: any, idx: number) => ({
            id: s.id || `ts_${p.id}_${idx}`,
            projectId: p.id,
            projectCode: p.code,
            projectStore: p.store,
            projectYear: p.year,
            name: s.name,
            start: s.start,
            finish: s.finish,
            orderIndex: s.orderIndex ?? idx,
            baselineStart: s.baselineStart || s.start,
            baselineFinish: s.baselineFinish || s.finish,
            approval: s.approval,
            party: s.party,
            taskCodePhu: s.taskCodePhu,
            parentTaskId: s.parentTaskId,
            duration: s.duration ?? 0,
            runningStatus: s.runningStatus,
            delegation: s.delegation,
          } as Task));

      const computedDates = calculateProjectComputedDates(p, sourceTasksForCalc, delegationGroups);
      const nextProj: Project = {
        ...p,
        ...computedDates,
        taskCount: useInMemoryTasks
          ? (computedDates.taskCount !== undefined ? computedDates.taskCount : pTasks.length)
          : existingSummaries.length,
        taskSummaries: useInMemoryTasks
          ? (computedDates.taskSummaries || [])
          : existingSummaries,
      };
      touchedProjectsToPersist.push(nextProj);
      return nextProj;
    });
    projectsChanged = true;
  }

  if (projectsChanged) store.setProjects(updatedProjects);
  if (tasksChanged) store.setTasks(updatedTasks);
  if (costsChanged) store.setCosts(updatedCosts);
  if (delegationsChanged) store.setDelegationGroups(updatedDelegations);

  if (touchedProjectsToPersist.length > 0) {
    localDb.projects.bulkPut(touchedProjectsToPersist).catch(() => {});
  }

  // Trụ cột 3: Kích hoạt Smart Change Flash cho các dòng vừa nhận thay đổi từ đồng nghiệp (Google Sheets standard)
  const flashEntries: Array<{ docId: string; collectionName: any; updatedBy?: string }> = [];
  deltas.forEach((d) => {
    if (d.action === 'batch_update' && Array.isArray(d.batchItems)) {
      d.batchItems.forEach((item) => {
        if (item.action === 'create' || item.action === 'update') {
          flashEntries.push({ docId: item.docId, collectionName: item.collectionName, updatedBy: d.updatedBy });
        }
      });
    } else if ((d.action === 'create' || d.action === 'update') && d.docId && d.collectionName) {
      flashEntries.push({ docId: d.docId, collectionName: d.collectionName, updatedBy: d.updatedBy });
    }
  });
  if (flashEntries.length > 0) {
    triggerBatchChangeFlash(flashEntries);
  }

  // Lưu các delta vào Dexie Local-First Database ngầm
  applyDeltasToLocalDatabase(deltas).catch(() => {});

  // Phát tín hiệu delta đã nạp sang các tab khác (giúp các tab phụ không cần gửi request lên Firestore)
  broadcastAppliedDeltas(deltas);

  // Cập nhật mốc đồng bộ mới nhất
  safeSetItemLocal(LAST_SYNC_KEY, String(maxTime));

  // Resolve signal-only (invalidate) items: re-read just those docs, then apply them like normal deltas
  if (invalidations.length > 0) {
    resolveInvalidations(invalidations)
      .then((synthetic) => {
        if (synthetic.length > 0) applyIncomingDeltas(synthetic);
      })
      .catch((err) => console.warn('[deltaSync] invalidate resolve failed:', err));
  }

  return deltas.length;
}
================ END FILE: src/lib/deltaApplyService.ts ================

================ FILE: src/lib/localDb.ts ================
import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import { Project, Task, Cost, DelegationGroup } from '../types';

export interface LocalSyncMeta {
  key: string;
  value: string;
  updatedAt: number;
}

/**
 * TakaLocalDB: IndexedDB Local-First Database bọc bởi Dexie.js
 * Cung cấp khả năng lưu trữ cục bộ có chỉ mục (indexed), truy vấn O(1) và nạp tức thì 0ms
 */
export class TakaLocalDB extends Dexie {
  projects!: Table<Project, string>;
  tasks!: Table<Task, string>;
  costs!: Table<Cost, string>;
  delegations!: Table<DelegationGroup, string>;
  syncMeta!: Table<LocalSyncMeta, string>;

  constructor() {
    super('TakaLocalFirstDB_v1');
    this.version(1).stores({
      projects: '&id, code, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      tasks: '&id, projectId, code, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      costs: '&id, projectId, code, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      delegations: '&id, projectId, code, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      syncMeta: '&key, updatedAt',
    });
    this.version(2).stores({
      projects: '&id, code, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      tasks: '&id, projectId, projectCode, code, projectStore, projectYear, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      costs: '&id, projectId, projectCode, code, projectStore, projectYear, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      delegations: '&id, projectId, projectCode, code, projectStore, projectYear, store, year, status, updatedAt',
      syncMeta: '&key, updatedAt',
    });
    this.version(3).stores({
      delegations: '&id, projectId, projectCode, parentTaskId, code, projectStore, projectYear, store, year, status, updatedAt',
    });
  }
}

import { isTaskDeletedInTracker } from './taskDeletedTracker';

const tsOf = (v: any): number => {
  if (!v) return 0;
  if (typeof v === 'string') {
    const n = Date.parse(v);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }
  if (typeof v?.toMillis === 'function') {
    const n = v.toMillis();
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }
  if (typeof v?.seconds === 'number') {
    return v.seconds * 1000 + Math.floor((v.nanoseconds || 0) / 1e6);
  }
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
};

export const localDb = new TakaLocalDB();

/**
 * Lưu hàng loạt Dự án vào Local Database (Dexie)
 */
export async function bulkPersistProjects(projects: Project[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!projects || projects.length === 0) return;
  try {
    await localDb.projects.bulkPut(projects);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] bulkPersistProjects error:', err);
  }
}

 * Lưu hàng loạt Tasks vào Local Database (Dexie)

export async function bulkPersistTasks(tasks: Task[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!tasks || tasks.length === 0) return;
  try {
    await localDb.tasks.bulkPut(tasks);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] bulkPersistTasks error:', err);
  }
}

export async function bulkPersistCosts(costs: Cost[]): Promise<void> {

    await localDb.costs.bulkPut(costs);
  } catch (err) {

}

/**
 * Cập nhật hoặc thêm mới 1 bản ghi vào bảng tương ứng trong Local Database
 */
export async function upsertLocalRecord(
  collectionName: 'taka_projects' | 'taka_tasks' | 'taka_costs' | 'taka_delegations',
  item: any
): Promise<void> {
  if (!item?.id) return;
  try {
    const table = (() => {
      switch (collectionName) {
        case 'taka_projects': return localDb.projects;
        case 'taka_tasks': return localDb.tasks;
        case 'taka_costs': return localDb.costs;
        case 'taka_delegations': return localDb.delegations;
      }
    })();
    
    // [Safety Guard] If item is marked as deleted, remove it from local cache
    if (item?.isDeleted) {
      await table.delete(item.id);
      return;
    }

    // [Safety Guard] Only upsert if the incoming doc is newer than what we have
    const existing = await table.get(item.id);
    const existingTs = tsOf(existing?.updatedAt);
    const incomingTs = tsOf(item.updatedAt);
    if (existing && existingTs > 0 && incomingTs > 0 && existingTs > incomingTs) return;

    await table.put(item);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[localDb] upsertLocalRecord error on ${collectionName}:`, err);
  }
}

/**
 * Áp dụng field-level patch vào Local Database (Dexie)
 * Nếu bản ghi đã tồn tại: chỉ cập nhật các trường thay đổi (giữ nguyên các trường còn lại)
 * Nếu chưa tồn tại: thêm mới bản ghi
 */
export async function patchLocalRecord(
  collectionName: 'taka_projects' | 'taka_tasks' | 'taka_costs' | 'taka_delegations',
  id: string,
  patch: Record<string, any>
): Promise<void> {
  if (!id || !patch) return;
  try {
    const table = (() => {
      switch (collectionName) {
        case 'taka_projects': return localDb.projects;
        case 'taka_tasks': return localDb.tasks;
        case 'taka_costs': return localDb.costs;
        case 'taka_delegations': return localDb.delegations;
      }
    })();
    
    // Patch only existing rows. Creating a row from a partial patch produces ghost/incomplete records
    const existing = await table.get(id);
    if (!existing) return;

    // If patch marks doc as deleted, delete it from local Dexie immediately
    if (patch?.isDeleted) {
      await table.delete(id);
      return;
    }

    // Only skip if incoming patch is explicitly older than existing (both timestamps valid)
    const existingTs = tsOf(existing.updatedAt);
    const incomingTs = tsOf(patch.updatedAt);
    if (existingTs > 0 && incomingTs > 0 && existingTs > incomingTs) return;

    await (table as any).update(id, patch);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[localDb] patchLocalRecord error on ${collectionName}:`, err);
  }
}

/**
 * Xóa 1 bản ghi khỏi Local Database
 */
export async function deleteLocalRecord(
  collectionName: 'taka_projects' | 'taka_tasks' | 'taka_costs' | 'taka_delegations',
  id: string
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    switch (collectionName) {
      case 'taka_projects':
        await localDb.projects.delete(id);
        break;
      case 'taka_tasks':
        await localDb.tasks.delete(id);
        break;
      case 'taka_costs':
        await localDb.costs.delete(id);
        break;
      case 'taka_delegations':
        await localDb.delegations.delete(id);
        break;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[localDb] deleteLocalRecord error on ${collectionName}:`, err);
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc toàn bộ Dự án từ Local Database theo bộ lọc
 */
export async function getLocalProjects(year?: string, store?: string): Promise<Project[]> {
  try {
    let collection = localDb.projects.toCollection();
    if (year && year !== 'all' && year !== 'ALL') {
      const yearKeys: Array<string | number> = [String(year)];
      if (Number.isFinite(Number(year))) yearKeys.push(Number(year));
      collection = localDb.projects.where('year').anyOf(yearKeys);
    }
    const results = await collection.toArray();
    const activeProjects = results.filter((p) => !p.isDeleted);
    if (store && store !== 'all' && store !== 'ALL') {
      return activeProjects.filter(
        (p) => p.store && p.store.toLowerCase() === store.toLowerCase()
      );
    }
    return activeProjects;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalProjects error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
/**
 * Đọc Tasks từ Local Database theo danh sách Project IDs hoặc Project Codes (hỗ trợ lọc theo year & store)
 */
export async function getLocalTasksForProjects(
  projectIdsOrCodes: string[],
  year?: string | number | null,
  store?: string | null
): Promise<Task[]> {
  if (!projectIdsOrCodes || projectIdsOrCodes.length === 0) return [];
  try {
    const rawIds = projectIdsOrCodes.map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (rawIds.length === 0) return [];
    const [byProjId, byProjCode, byCode] = await Promise.all([
      localDb.tasks.where('projectId').anyOf(rawIds).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.tasks.where('projectCode').anyOf(rawIds).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.tasks.where('code').anyOf(rawIds).toArray().catch(() => []),
    ]);

    const normYear = year !== undefined && year !== null && String(year).toLowerCase() !== 'all'
      ? String(year).trim()
      : '';
    const normStore = store && String(store).toLowerCase() !== 'all'
      ? String(store).trim().toUpperCase()
      : '';

    const matchesScope = (t: Task, matchedById: boolean): boolean => {
      if (!t || !t.id || t.isDeleted) return false;
      if (isTaskDeletedInTracker(t.id, t.projectId ? String(t.projectId) : undefined)) return false;
      const tYear = t.projectYear !== undefined && t.projectYear !== null ? String(t.projectYear).trim() : '';
      const tStore = t.projectStore ? String(t.projectStore).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
      if (normYear && tYear && tYear !== normYear) return false;
      if (normStore && tStore && tStore !== normStore) return false;
      if (!matchedById && t.projectId && rawIds.length > 0) {
        // If rawIds contains a project UUID and t.projectId points to a DIFFERENT UUID while missing year/store, guard against cross-year collision
        const hasUuidInQuery = rawIds.some(k => k.length >= 16 && !k.includes(' '));
        if (hasUuidInQuery && !rawIds.includes(String(t.projectId).trim())) {
          if (!normYear || !tYear || !normStore || !tStore) return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    };

    const mergedMap = new Map<string, Task>();
    for (const t of byProjId) {
      if (matchesScope(t, true)) mergedMap.set(t.id, t);
    }
    for (const t of byProjCode) {
      if (matchesScope(t, false)) mergedMap.set(t.id, t);
    }
    for (const t of byCode) {
      if (matchesScope(t, false)) mergedMap.set(t.id, t);
    }
    return Array.from(mergedMap.values());
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalTasksForProjects error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc toàn bộ Tasks từ Local Database
 */
export async function getLocalTasks(year?: string): Promise<Task[]> {
  try {
    if (!year || year === 'all' || year === 'ALL') {
      const all = await localDb.tasks.toArray();
      return all.filter(t => !t.isDeleted && !isTaskDeletedInTracker(t.id, t.projectId ? String(t.projectId) : undefined));
    }
    const strYear = String(year);
    const [byYear, byProjYear] = await Promise.all([
      localDb.tasks.where('year').anyOf(Number.isFinite(Number(strYear)) ? [strYear, Number(strYear)] : [strYear]).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.tasks.where('projectYear').anyOf(Number.isFinite(Number(strYear)) ? [strYear, Number(strYear)] : [strYear]).toArray().catch(() => []),
    ]);
    const merged = new Map<string, Task>();
    for (const t of byYear) {
      if (t && t.id && !t.isDeleted && !isTaskDeletedInTracker(t.id, t.projectId ? String(t.projectId) : undefined)) {
        merged.set(t.id, t);
      }
    }
    for (const t of byProjYear) {
      if (t && t.id && !t.isDeleted && !isTaskDeletedInTracker(t.id, t.projectId ? String(t.projectId) : undefined)) {
        merged.set(t.id, t);
      }
    }
    return Array.from(merged.values());
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalTasks error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc toàn bộ Costs từ Local Database
 */
export async function getLocalCosts(year?: string): Promise<Cost[]> {
  try {
    if (!year || year === 'all' || year === 'ALL') {
      const all = await localDb.costs.toArray();
      return all.filter((c) => !c.isDeleted);
    }
    const strYear = String(year);
    const [byYear, byProjYear] = await Promise.all([
      localDb.costs.where('year').anyOf(Number.isFinite(Number(strYear)) ? [strYear, Number(strYear)] : [strYear]).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.costs.where('projectYear').anyOf(Number.isFinite(Number(strYear)) ? [strYear, Number(strYear)] : [strYear]).toArray().catch(() => []),
    ]);
    const merged = new Map<string, Cost>();
    for (const c of byYear) if (c && c.id && !c.isDeleted) merged.set(c.id, c);
    for (const c of byProjYear) if (c && c.id && !c.isDeleted) merged.set(c.id, c);
    return Array.from(merged.values());
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalCosts error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc toàn bộ Delegations từ Local Database (hỗ trợ lọc theo năm)
 */
export async function getLocalDelegations(year?: string): Promise<DelegationGroup[]> {
  try {
    if (!year || year === 'all' || year === 'ALL') {
      const all = await localDb.delegations.toArray();
      return all.filter((d) => !d.isDeleted);
    }
    const strYear = String(year);
    const numYear = Number(year);
    const queries = [
      localDb.delegations.where('year').equals(strYear).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.delegations.where('projectYear').equals(strYear).toArray().catch(() => []),
    ];
    if (!isNaN(numYear)) {
      queries.push(
        localDb.delegations.where('projectYear').equals(numYear).toArray().catch(() => [])
      );
    }
    const results = await Promise.all(queries);
    const merged = new Map<string, DelegationGroup>();
    for (const list of results) {
      for (const d of list) {
        if (d && d.id && !d.isDeleted) merged.set(d.id, d);
      }
    }
    return Array.from(merged.values());
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalDelegations error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc Delegations từ Local Database theo danh sách Project IDs hoặc Project Codes (hỗ trợ lọc theo year & store)
 */
export async function getLocalDelegationsForProjects(
  projectIdsOrCodes: string[],
  year?: string | number | null,
  store?: string | null
): Promise<DelegationGroup[]> {
  if (!projectIdsOrCodes || projectIdsOrCodes.length === 0) return [];
  try {
    const rawKeys = projectIdsOrCodes.map(k => String(k).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (rawKeys.length === 0) return [];

    const [byProjCode, byProjId, byCode] = await Promise.all([
      localDb.delegations.where('projectCode').anyOf(rawKeys).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.delegations.where('projectId').anyOf(rawKeys).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.delegations.where('code').anyOf(rawKeys).toArray().catch(() => []),
    ]);

    const normYear = year !== undefined && year !== null && String(year).toLowerCase() !== 'all'
      ? String(year).trim()
      : '';
    const normStore = store && String(store).toLowerCase() !== 'all'
      ? String(store).trim().toUpperCase()
      : '';

    const matchesScope = (d: DelegationGroup, matchedById: boolean): boolean => {
      if (!d || !d.id || d.isDeleted) return false;
      const dYear = d.projectYear !== undefined && d.projectYear !== null ? String(d.projectYear).trim() : '';
      const dStore = d.projectStore ? String(d.projectStore).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
      if (normYear && dYear && dYear !== normYear) return false;
      if (normStore && dStore && dStore !== normStore) return false;
      if (!matchedById && d.projectId && rawKeys.length > 0) {
        const hasUuidInQuery = rawKeys.some(k => k.length >= 16 && !k.includes(' '));
        if (hasUuidInQuery && !rawKeys.includes(String(d.projectId).trim())) {
          if (!normYear || !dYear || !normStore || !dStore) return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    };

    const merged = new Map<string, DelegationGroup>();
    for (const d of byProjId) if (matchesScope(d, true)) merged.set(d.id, d);
    for (const d of byProjCode) if (matchesScope(d, false)) merged.set(d.id, d);
    for (const d of byCode) if (matchesScope(d, false)) merged.set(d.id, d);

    return Array.from(merged.values());
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalDelegationsForProjects error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc Delegations từ Local Database theo danh sách Task IDs
 */
export async function getLocalDelegationsForTasks(taskIds: string[]): Promise<DelegationGroup[]> {
  if (!taskIds || taskIds.length === 0) return [];
  try {
    const rawIds = taskIds.map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (rawIds.length === 0) return [];

    const [byParentTaskId, byId] = await Promise.all([
      localDb.delegations.where('parentTaskId').anyOf(rawIds).toArray().catch(() => []),
      localDb.delegations.where('id').anyOf(rawIds).toArray().catch(() => []),
    ]);

    const merged = new Map<string, DelegationGroup>();
    for (const d of byParentTaskId) if (d && d.id && !d.isDeleted) merged.set(d.id, d);
    for (const d of byId) if (d && d.id && !d.isDeleted) merged.set(d.id, d);

    return Array.from(merged.values());
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] getLocalDelegationsForTasks error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Đọc giá trị metadata đồng bộ
 */
export async function getLocalSyncMeta(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const record = await localDb.syncMeta.get(key);
    return record ? record.value : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Ghi giá trị metadata đồng bộ
 */
export async function setLocalSyncMeta(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await localDb.syncMeta.put({
      key,
      value,
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] setLocalSyncMeta error:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Xóa sạch toàn bộ dữ liệu Dexie IndexedDB (dành cho Resync Cache)
 */
export async function clearDexieDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await Promise.all([
      localDb.projects.clear().catch(() => {}),
      localDb.tasks.clear().catch(() => {}),
      localDb.costs.clear().catch(() => {}),
      localDb.delegations.clear().catch(() => {}),
      localDb.syncMeta.clear().catch(() => {}),
    ]);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[localDb] clearDexieDatabase error:', err);
  }
}
