// Delta-first: sau lần tải đầy đủ đầu tiên, hủy onSnapshot lớn, dựa vào Dexie + kênh delta.
// Bật TỪNG CÁI sau khi đã test. Mặc định tắt = hành vi hiện tại.
const DELTA_FIRST = { projects: false, tasks: false, costs: false, delegations: false };

interface UseProjectsHydrationProps {
  querySignature: string | null;
  userRole?: string;
  allowedStores: string[];
}

// Chuyển updatedAt (ISO string / Firestore Timestamp / plain {seconds} / number) về ms
const tsOf = (v: any): number => {
  if (!v) return 0;
  if (typeof v === "string") {
    const n = Date.parse(v);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }
  if (typeof v?.toMillis === "function") {
    const n = v.toMillis();
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }
  if (typeof v?.seconds === "number") {
    return v.seconds * 1000 + Math.floor((v.nanoseconds || 0) / 1e6);
  }
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
};

// Snapshot lấy từ cache cục bộ có thể cũ hơn dữ liệu đã nhận qua delta -> không cho ghi đè.
// Chỉ áp dụng cho snapshot fromCache; snapshot từ server luôn được tin (tránh lệch đồng hồ)
const isStaleCacheDoc = (existing: any, incoming: any): boolean => {
  if (!existing) return false;
  const e = tsOf(existing.updatedAt);
  const i = tsOf(incoming?.updatedAt);
  return e > 0 && i > 0 && i < e;
};

// Các (người dùng, bộ lọc store, năm) thuộc nhóm năm phụ đã được nạp trong phiên này: không truy vấn server lặp lại
const secondaryYearsFetched = new Set<string>();

export function useProjectsHydration({
  querySignature,
  userRole,
  allowedStores,
}: UseProjectsHydrationProps) {
  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalYearFilter);
  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setCloudStatus = useAppStore((state) => state.setCloudStatus);
  const [recoveryTick, setRecoveryTick] = useState(0);

  const projectsPool = useRef<Map<string, () => void>>(new Map());
  const prevSigProjects = useRef<string>("");
  const remoteYearsMetaRef = useRef<YearsMetaMap>({});

  // Atomic batching buffers for Projects and Tasks updates to prevent JS main thread freezing
  const pendingProjectsMap = useRef<Map<string, Project> | null>(null);
  const pendingProjectsChanged = useRef<Map<string, Project>>(new Map());
  const pendingProjectsRemoved = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const pendingDeletedTaskIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const pendingInvalidatedTaskIds = useRef<Map<string, string>>(new Map()); // taskId -> year
  const projectsBatchTimer = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  const batchProjectsUpdate = useCallback((docChanges: any[], fromCache: boolean = false) => {
    if (!pendingProjectsMap.current) {
      const currentProjects = useAppStore.getState().projects || [];
      const map = new Map<string, Project>();
      currentProjects.forEach(p => map.set(p.id, p));
      pendingProjectsMap.current = map;
    }

    const storeState = useAppStore.getState();
    const currentTasks = storeState.tasks || [];
    const expandedSet = new Set([
      ...(storeState.expandedProjectIds || []),
      ...(storeState.searchActiveProjectIds || []),
    ].map(String));
    const nowMs = Date.now();

    docChanges.forEach((change) => {
      const id = change.doc.id;
      const rawDocData = change.doc.data();
      const data = { id, ...rawDocData } as Project;
      const isTrueDelete = Boolean(data.isDeleted || change.type === "removed");

      if (isTrueDelete) {
        pendingProjectsMap.current!.delete(id);
        pendingProjectsChanged.current.delete(id);
        pendingProjectsRemoved.current.add(id);
      } else {
        if (fromCache && isStaleCacheDoc(pendingProjectsMap.current!.get(id), data)) return;
        const existing = pendingProjectsMap.current!.get(id);
        const hasPendingWrites = Boolean(change.doc?.metadata?.hasPendingWrites);

        const resolvedSummaries = data.taskSummaries !== undefined ? data.taskSummaries : (existing?.taskSummaries || []);
        const resolvedCount = Array.isArray(data.taskSummaries) && data.taskSummaries.length > 0
          ? data.taskSummaries.length
          : (data.taskCount !== undefined ? data.taskCount : (existing?.taskCount ?? 0));

        const resolvedData: Project = existing ? {
          ...existing,
          ...data,
          taskCount: resolvedCount,
          taskSummaries: resolvedSummaries,
        } : {
          ...data,
          taskCount: resolvedCount,
          taskSummaries: resolvedSummaries,
        };

        // Server-to-Device Task Summary Reconciliation:
        // Compare authoritative server project.taskSummaries with local tasks (Zustand + Dexie)
        if (!fromCache && !hasPendingWrites && Array.isArray(data.taskSummaries)) {
          const serverSummaryIds = new Set(
            data.taskSummaries.map((s: any) => String(s?.id || "").trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean)
          );
          const isZeroTaskServer = data.taskSummaries.length === 0 && (data.taskCount ?? 0) === 0;

          if (serverSummaryIds.size > 0 || isZeroTaskServer) {
            // 1. Any task ID that was in existing.taskSummaries but is no longer in server data.taskSummaries was deleted on another device
            if (existing && Array.isArray(existing.taskSummaries)) {
              existing.taskSummaries.forEach((oldS: any) => {
                const oldId = String(oldS?.id || "").trim();
                if (oldId && !serverSummaryIds.has(oldId.toLowerCase())) {
                  markTasksDeleted([oldId], id);
                  pendingDeletedTaskIds.current.add(oldId);
                }
              });
            }

            // 2. Any in-memory task belonging to this project that is not in serverSummaryIds (and not edited in last 30s) is a zombie task
            const localProjTasks = currentTasks.filter(t => isTaskBelongsToProject(t, resolvedData));
            const localProjTasksById = new Map<string, any>();
            localProjTasks.forEach((t) => {
              if (!t?.id || String(t.id).startsWith("ts_")) return;
              const tIdLower = String(t.id).trim().toLowerCase();
              localProjTasksById.set(tIdLower, t);
              if (!serverSummaryIds.has(tIdLower)) {
                const updatedMs = tsOf(t.updatedAt);
                if (!updatedMs || nowMs - updatedMs > 30000) {
                  markTasksDeleted([t.id], id);
                  pendingDeletedTaskIds.current.add(t.id);
                }
              }
            });

            // 3. If project already has tasks hydrated in RAM (or is expanded), check if server data.taskSummaries has added or modified tasks
            const isProjHydratedOrExpanded =
              localProjTasks.length > 0 ||
              expandedSet.has(String(id)) ||
              (resolvedData.code && expandedSet.has(String(resolvedData.code)));

            if (isProjHydratedOrExpanded && serverSummaryIds.size > 0) {
              data.taskSummaries.forEach((s: any) => {
                const sId = String(s?.id || "").trim();
                if (!sId || sId.startsWith("ts_")) return;
                const localT = localProjTasksById.get(sId.toLowerCase());
                if (!localT) {
                  pendingInvalidatedTaskIds.current.set(sId, String(resolvedData.year || ""));
                } else {
                  const localUpdatedMs = tsOf(localT.updatedAt);
                  const isRecentlyEditedLocally = localUpdatedMs > 0 && nowMs - localUpdatedMs <= 30000;
                  if (!isRecentlyEditedLocally) {
                    const summaryDiffers =
                      (s.name !== undefined && s.name !== localT.name) ||
                      (s.start !== undefined && s.start !== localT.start) ||
                      (s.finish !== undefined && s.finish !== localT.finish) ||
                      (s.runningStatus !== undefined && s.runningStatus !== localT.runningStatus) ||
                      (s.approval !== undefined && s.approval !== localT.approval) ||
                      (s.party !== undefined && s.party !== localT.party);
                    if (summaryDiffers) {
                      pendingInvalidatedTaskIds.current.set(sId, String(resolvedData.year || ""));
                    }
                  }
                }
              });
            }
          }
        }

        pendingProjectsMap.current!.set(id, resolvedData);
        pendingProjectsChanged.current.set(id, resolvedData);
        pendingProjectsRemoved.current.delete(id);
      }
    });

    if (projectsBatchTimer.current) {
      clearTimeout(projectsBatchTimer.current);
    }

    projectsBatchTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      if (pendingProjectsMap.current) {
        const updatedList = Array.from(pendingProjectsMap.current.values());
        updatedList.sort((a, b) => (a.no || 0) - (b.no || 0));
        useAppStore.getState().setProjects(updatedList);

        if (pendingDeletedTaskIds.current.size > 0) {
          const delIdsLower = new Set(
            Array.from(pendingDeletedTaskIds.current).map((tid) => String(tid).trim().toLowerCase())
          );
          const delRawIds = Array.from(pendingDeletedTaskIds.current);
          pendingDeletedTaskIds.current.clear();

          const latestTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
          const filteredTasks = latestTasks.filter(
            (t) => !t?.id || !delIdsLower.has(String(t.id).trim().toLowerCase())
          );
          if (filteredTasks.length !== latestTasks.length) {
            useAppStore.getState().setTasks(filteredTasks);
          }
          delRawIds.forEach((tid) => {
            deleteLocalRecord('taka_tasks', tid).catch(() => {});
          });
        }

        if (pendingInvalidatedTaskIds.current.size > 0) {
          const itemsToResolve: TakaDeltaItem[] = [];
          pendingInvalidatedTaskIds.current.forEach((yr, tid) => {
            itemsToResolve.push({
              docId: tid,
              collectionName: 'taka_tasks',
              action: 'update',
              data: {},
              isPatch: true,
              invalidate: true,
              year: yr,
            });
          });
          pendingInvalidatedTaskIds.current.clear();
          resolveInvalidations(itemsToResolve)
            .then((synthetic) => {
              if (synthetic.length > 0) applyIncomingDeltas(synthetic);
            })
            .catch(() => {});
        }

        // Chỉ ghi Dexie phần thay đổi; xóa khỏi Dexie phần đã bị xóa (chống "sống lại")
        const changed = Array.from(pendingProjectsChanged.current.values());
        const removed = Array.from(pendingProjectsRemoved.current);
        pendingProjectsChanged.current.clear();
        pendingProjectsRemoved.current.clear();
        if (changed.length > 0) {
          offloadPersistSnapshot('taka_projects', changed);

          // Background Dexie Task Reconciliation: even when on General tab (store.tasks is empty),
          // purge any zombie tasks in localDb.tasks that were deleted on another device according to server project.taskSummaries!
          if (!fromCache) {
            setTimeout(async () => {
              try {
                const nowBg = Date.now();
                for (const p of changed) {
                  if (!p?.id || !Array.isArray(p.taskSummaries)) continue;
                  const summaryIds = new Set(
                    p.taskSummaries.map((s: any) => String(s?.id || "").trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean)
                  );
                  const isZeroTask = p.taskSummaries.length === 0 && (p.taskCount ?? 0) === 0;
                  if (summaryIds.size === 0 && !isZeroTask) continue;

                  const summaryById = new Map<string, any>();
                  p.taskSummaries.forEach((s: any) => {
                    if (s?.id) summaryById.set(String(s.id).trim().toLowerCase(), s);
                  });

                  const keys = [String(p.id)];
                  if (p.code) keys.push(String(p.code).trim());
                  if (p.linkCodes) {
                    String(p.linkCodes).split(/[,;/]+/).forEach(c => {
                      if (c.trim()) keys.push(c.trim());
                    });
                  }
                  const [byId, byCode] = await Promise.all([
                    localDb.tasks.where('projectId').equals(String(p.id)).toArray().catch(() => []),
                    localDb.tasks.where('projectCode').anyOf(keys).toArray().catch(() => []),
                  ]);
                  const candidateMap = new Map<string, any>();
                  byId.forEach(t => { if (t?.id) candidateMap.set(t.id, t); });
                  byCode.forEach(t => { if (t?.id && isTaskBelongsToProject(t, p)) candidateMap.set(t.id, t); });

                  for (const [tid, t] of candidateMap.entries()) {
                    const tidLower = String(tid).trim().toLowerCase();
                    const tUpdatedMs = tsOf(t.updatedAt);
                    if (tUpdatedMs > 0 && nowBg - tUpdatedMs <= 30000) continue;

                    if (!summaryIds.has(tidLower)) {
                      markTasksDeleted([tid], p.id);
                      await deleteLocalRecord('taka_tasks', tid);
                      const curTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
                      if (curTasks.some(ct => String(ct.id).trim().toLowerCase() === tidLower)) {
                        useAppStore.getState().setTasks(
                          curTasks.filter(ct => String(ct.id).trim().toLowerCase() !== tidLower)
                        );
                      }
                    } else {
                      const s = summaryById.get(tidLower);
                      if (s) {
                        const needsPatch =
                          (s.name !== undefined && s.name !== t.name) ||
                          (s.start !== undefined && s.start !== t.start) ||
                          (s.finish !== undefined && s.finish !== t.finish) ||
                          (s.runningStatus !== undefined && s.runningStatus !== t.runningStatus);
                        if (needsPatch) {
                          await patchLocalRecord('taka_tasks', tid, {
                            name: s.name ?? t.name,
                            start: s.start ?? t.start,
                            finish: s.finish ?? t.finish,
                            ...(s.runningStatus !== undefined ? { runningStatus: s.runningStatus } : {}),
                            ...(s.duration !== undefined ? { duration: s.duration } : {}),
                            ...(s.orderIndex !== undefined ? { orderIndex: s.orderIndex } : {}),
                          });
                        }
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              } catch {}
            }, 60);
          }
        }
        removed.forEach((id) => { deleteLocalRecord('taka_projects', id).catch(() => {}); });
        const setNotifs = useAppStore.getState().setAllProjectsForNotifs;
        if (setNotifs) setNotifs(updatedList);
        pendingProjectsMap.current = null;
      }
    }, 40);
  }, []);

  // Stage 3 & Delta Pipeline Startup
  useEffect(() => {
    bootstrapLocalStore().catch(() => {});

    fetchYearsMeta().then((meta) => {
      remoteYearsMetaRef.current = meta;
      setMemoryYearsMeta(meta);
    });

    const stopDeltaSync = startDeltaSyncService();
    return () => {
      stopDeltaSync();
    };
  }, []);

  // Sleep/Wake & Network Recovery Listener
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleSleepWakeRecovery = () => {
      // Sau khi thức giấc từ chế độ ngủ hoặc khôi phục mạng:
      // Hủy các listener cũ (hoặc dummy listener) trong projectsPool và tăng recoveryTick
      // để useEffect chính gắn lại listener mới và đối soát (reconcile) snapshot trực tiếp với server!
      projectsPool.current.forEach((u, key) => {
        try { u(); } catch {}
        usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_projects_${key}`);
      });
      projectsPool.current.clear();
      secondaryYearsFetched.clear();
      setRecoveryTick((c) => c + 1);
    };

    window.addEventListener("taka_sleep_wake_recovery", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
    window.addEventListener("taka_network_recovered", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("taka_sleep_wake_recovery", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
      window.removeEventListener("taka_network_recovered", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
    };
  }, []);

  // Unmount cleanup
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      projectsPool.current.forEach((u, key) => {
        u();
        usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_projects_${key}`);
      });
      projectsPool.current.clear();
      if (projectsBatchTimer.current) clearTimeout(projectsBatchTimer.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // Main Unified Global Delta Sync for Projects
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!querySignature) return;

    if (prevSigProjects.current !== querySignature) {
      projectsPool.current.forEach((u, key) => {
        u();
        usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_projects_${key}`);
      });
      projectsPool.current.clear();
      prevSigProjects.current = querySignature;
    }

    const isAllYears = globalYearFilter === "all" || globalYearFilter === "ALL";
    const liveTargetYears = isAllYears ? PRIMARY_ACTIVE_YEARS : [globalYearFilter];

    liveTargetYears.forEach(async (year) => {
      // LUÔN đảm bảo nạp lại dữ liệu từ Dexie Local-First khi người dùng đổi bộ lọc Year hoặc Store,
      // kể cả khi listener của năm đó đã nằm trong projectsPool!
      const localCount = await hydrateProjectsFromLocal(year, globalStoreFilter);

      if (!projectsPool.current.has(year)) {
        // Chỉ coi các năm cũ hơn năm ngoái (vd <= 2024) là năm đã đóng sổ hoàn toàn
        const isClosedOrPastYear = Number(year) < new Date().getFullYear() - 1;
        const hasLocalData =
          localCount > 0 ||
          (useAppStore.getState().projects || []).some((p) => String(p.year) === String(year));

        const deltaManaged =
          (hasLocalData && isClosedOrPastYear) ||
          (DELTA_FIRST.projects && hasLocalData && hasMarker(await getLoadedMarkers(), 'projects_year', year));
        if (deltaManaged) {
          projectsPool.current.set(year, () => {});
          pullAndApplyDeltas(year, true)
            .then(() => setCloudStatus("online"))
            .catch(() => {});
          usePerformanceStore
            .getState()
            .trackGetDoc(
              `taka_projects_${year}`,
              true,
              localCount,
              "Dexie Local-First Projects Hydration (0 Read full collection)"
            );
          return;
        }

        if (false && isClosedOrPastYear && isYearCacheValid(year, remoteYearsMetaRef.current)) {
          const cached = loadYearDataFromLocal(year);
          if (cached && cached.projects && cached.projects.length > 0) {
            batchProjectsUpdate(
              cached.projects.map((p) => ({
                type: "added",
                doc: { id: p.id, data: () => p },
              }))
            );
            usePerformanceStore
              .getState()
              .trackGetDoc(
                `taka_projects_${year}`,
                true,
                cached.projects.length,
                "Metadata Fingerprint Cache Hit"
              );
            projectsPool.current.set(year, () => {});
            return;
          }
        }

        const colRef = collection(db, "artifacts", APP_ID, "public", "data", "taka_projects");
        const qProjects = buildStoreAndYearQuery(colRef, "store", "year", year, userRole, allowedStores);

        usePerformanceStore.getState().registerListener(`taka_projects_${year}`);
        const unsub = onSnapshot(
          qProjects,
          (snapshot) => {
            usePerformanceStore.getState().trackSnapshot(`taka_projects_${year}`, snapshot);
            const fromCache = snapshot.metadata.fromCache;
            let changesToApply: any[] = snapshot.docChanges();

            // Server snapshot reconciliation: purge any zombie project in Dexie/Zustand for this year that no longer exists on server
            if (!fromCache && !snapshot.metadata.hasPendingWrites) {
              const serverIds = new Set<string>();
              snapshot.docs.forEach((d) => serverIds.add(d.id));
              const currentProjectsNow = useAppStore.getState().projects || [];
              const nowMs = Date.now();
              const allowedLower = allowedStores.map((s) => s.toLowerCase());
              const isStoreRestricted = userRole !== "admin" && allowedLower.length > 0 && !allowedLower.includes("all");
              const zombieRemovals: any[] = [];
              currentProjectsNow.forEach((p) => {
                if (
                  p?.id &&
                  String(p.year ?? "").trim() === String(year).trim() &&
                  !serverIds.has(p.id)
                ) {
                  if (isStoreRestricted && p.store && !allowedLower.includes(String(p.store).trim().toLowerCase())) {
                    return;
                  }
                  const updatedMs = tsOf(p.updatedAt);
                  if (!updatedMs || nowMs - updatedMs > 30000) {
                    zombieRemovals.push({
                      type: "removed",
                      doc: { id: p.id, data: () => ({ ...p, isDeleted: true }) },
                    });
                  }
                }
              });
              if (zombieRemovals.length > 0) {
                changesToApply = [...changesToApply, ...zombieRemovals];
              }
            }

            batchProjectsUpdate(changesToApply, fromCache);
            setCloudStatus("online");

            if (DELTA_FIRST.projects && !fromCache) {
              markLoaded('projects_year', year).catch(() => {});
              const u = projectsPool.current.get(year);
              projectsPool.current.set(year, () => {});
              u?.();
              usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_projects_${year}`);
            }
          },
          (e) => {
            projectsPool.current.delete(year);
            usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_projects_${year}`);
            console.error("Projects listener error:", e);
            setCloudStatus("offline");
          }
        );
        projectsPool.current.set(year, unsub);
      }
    });

    if (isAllYears) {
      SECONDARY_ACTIVE_YEARS.forEach(async (year) => {
        if (projectsPool.current.has(year)) return;
        const secKey = `${querySignature}_${globalStoreFilter}_${year}`;
        if (secondaryYearsFetched.has(secKey)) return;
        secondaryYearsFetched.add(secKey);
        try {
          const colRef = collection(db, "artifacts", APP_ID, "public", "data", "taka_projects");
          const qProjects = buildStoreAndYearQuery(colRef, "store", "year", year, userRole, allowedStores);
          let snapshot;
          try {
            snapshot = await getDocsFromCache(qProjects);
            if (snapshot.empty) {
              snapshot = await getDocsFromServer(qProjects);
            }
          } catch {
            snapshot = await getDocsFromServer(qProjects);
          }

          if (snapshot && !snapshot.empty) {
            const currentProjects = useAppStore.getState().projects || [];
            const projectMap = new Map<string, Project>();
            currentProjects.forEach((p) => projectMap.set(p.id, p));

            snapshot.forEach((docSnap) => {
              const data = { id: docSnap.id, ...docSnap.data() } as Project;
              if (!data.isDeleted) {
                projectMap.set(docSnap.id, data);
              }
            });

            const updatedList = Array.from(projectMap.values());
            updatedList.sort((a, b) => (a.no || 0) - (b.no || 0));
            useAppStore.getState().setProjects(updatedList);
          }
        } catch {
          // Lỗi mạng hoặc cache: cho phép thử lại ở lần chạy sau
          secondaryYearsFetched.delete(secKey);
        }
      });
    }
  }, [querySignature, userRole, allowedStores, globalYearFilter, globalStoreFilter, batchProjectsUpdate, setCloudStatus, recoveryTick]);
}
================ END FILE: src/hooks/useFirebaseDataParts/useProjectsHydration.ts ================

================ FILE: src/hooks/useFirebaseDataParts/useTasksHydration.ts ================
import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import { collection, onSnapshot, query, where } from "firebase/firestore";
import { useAppStore } from "../../store/useAppStore";
import { usePerformanceStore } from "../../store/usePerformanceStore";
import { db, APP_ID } from "../../lib/firebase";
import { Task } from "../../types";
import { buildStoreAndYearQuery } from "./firebaseQueryHelpers";
import {
  offloadPersistSnapshot,
  hydrateTasksFromLocal,
  hydrateTasksForProjects,
  hydrateDelegationsForProjects,
} from "../../lib/backgroundSyncWorker";
import { loadNotificationTasks } from "../../lib/notificationTaskService";
import { deduplicateTasksInMemory } from "../../lib/taskDeduplicationService";
import { cleanOrphanTasks } from "../../lib/taskStateService";
import { rollupParentTaskDatesAndStatus } from "../../lib/taskRollupService";
import { deleteLocalRecord } from "../../lib/localDb";
import { pullAndApplyDeltas } from "../../lib/deltaSyncService";
import { getLoadedMarkers, hasMarker, markLoaded } from "../../lib/loadedMarkers";
import {
  getAppBootAgeMs,
  BOOT_SYNC_GUARD_MS,
  calculateProjectComputedDates,
  syncProjectDatesToFirestore,
} from "../../lib/projectDatesService";
import { markTasksDeleted, unmarkTaskDeleted, clearDeletedTasksTracker } from "../../lib/taskDeletedTracker";

// Delta-first: sau lần tải đầy đủ đầu tiên, hủy onSnapshot lớn, dựa vào Dexie + kênh delta.
// Bật TỪNG CÁI sau khi đã test. Mặc định tắt = hành vi hiện tại.
const DELTA_FIRST = { projects: false, tasks: false, costs: false, delegations: false };


interface UseTasksHydrationProps {
  querySignature: string | null;
  userRole?: string;
  allowedStores: string[];
}

const TTL_DURATION_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes

import {
  isStaleCacheDoc,
  extractKeysToHydrate,
  groupActiveProjectsByYear,
  doesTaskBelongToProject,
  filterCompatibleProjectChanges,
  buildZombieTaskRemovalsForYear,
  buildZombieTaskRemovalsForProject,
  healProjectsAfterTasksBatch,
} from "./tasksHydrationHelpers";

export function useTasksHydration({
  querySignature,
  userRole,
  allowedStores,
}: UseTasksHydrationProps) {
  const isTasksRequested = useAppStore((state) => state.isTasksRequested);
  const expandedProjectIds = useAppStore((state) => state.expandedProjectIds);
  const searchActiveProjectIds = useAppStore((state) => state.searchActiveProjectIds);
  const visibleActiveProjectIds = useAppStore((state) => state.visibleActiveProjectIds);
  const visibleActiveProjectIdsRef = useRef(visibleActiveProjectIds);
  visibleActiveProjectIdsRef.current = visibleActiveProjectIds;

  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setIsTasksLoading = useAppStore((state) => state.setIsTasksLoading);
  const setAllTasksForNotifs = useAppStore((state) => state.setAllTasksForNotifs);
  const setCloudStatus = useAppStore((state) => state.setCloudStatus);

  const tasksPool = useRef<Map<string, () => void>>(new Map());
  const tasksTTLTimeouts = useRef<Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>>(new Map());
  const knownHydratedProjectCodes = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const wakeRefreshCounter = useRef<number>(0);

  const prevQuerySigTasks = useRef<string>("");
  const prevSigTasks = useRef<string>("");

  // Atomic batching buffers for Tasks updates
  const pendingTasksMap = useRef<Map<string, Task> | null>(null);
  const pendingTasksChanged = useRef<Map<string, Task>>(new Map());
  const pendingTasksRemoved = useRef<Map<string, Task>>(new Map());
  const tasksBatchTimer = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  const batchTasksUpdate = useCallback((docChanges: any[], fromCache: boolean = false, isForceSnapshot: boolean = false) => {
    if (!pendingTasksMap.current) {
      const currentTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
      const map = new Map<string, Task>();
      currentTasks.forEach((t) => map.set(t.id, t));
      pendingTasksMap.current = map;
    }

    docChanges.forEach((change) => {
      const id = change.doc.id;
      const rawDocData = change.doc.data();
      const data = { id, ...rawDocData } as Task;
      const isTrueDelete = Boolean(data.isDeleted || change.type === "removed");

      if (isTrueDelete) {
        const existing = pendingTasksMap.current!.get(id);
        const pIdHint = data.projectId || existing?.projectId;
        markTasksDeleted([id], pIdHint ? String(pIdHint) : undefined);
        pendingTasksMap.current!.delete(id);
        pendingTasksChanged.current.delete(id);
        pendingTasksRemoved.current.set(id, { ...(existing || {}), ...data, id });
      } else {
        if (!isForceSnapshot && fromCache && isStaleCacheDoc(pendingTasksMap.current!.get(id), data)) return;
        if (!data.isDeleted) {
          unmarkTaskDeleted(id);
        }
        pendingTasksMap.current!.set(id, data);
        pendingTasksChanged.current.set(id, data);
        pendingTasksRemoved.current.delete(id);
      }
    });

    if (tasksBatchTimer.current) {
      clearTimeout(tasksBatchTimer.current);
    }

    tasksBatchTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      if (pendingTasksMap.current) {
        const rawList = Array.from(pendingTasksMap.current.values());
        const projects = useAppStore.getState().projects || [];
        const { dedupedTasks } = deduplicateTasksInMemory(rawList, projects);
        const { cleaned: unrolledTasks } = cleanOrphanTasks(dedupedTasks);
        const rolledUpTasks = rollupParentTaskDatesAndStatus(unrolledTasks, useAppStore.getState().delegationGroups);
        rolledUpTasks.sort((a, b) => {
          if (a.projectCode !== b.projectCode) {
            return (a.projectCode || "").localeCompare(b.projectCode || "");
          }
          return (a.orderIndex || 0) - (b.orderIndex || 0);
        });
        const finalTasks = rolledUpTasks;
        useAppStore.getState().setTasks(finalTasks);

        const healedProjects = healProjectsAfterTasksBatch({
          currentProjects: useAppStore.getState().projects || [],
          finalTasks,
          delegationGroups: useAppStore.getState().delegationGroups || [],
          changedTasks: pendingTasksChanged.current,
          removedTasks: pendingTasksRemoved.current,
          fromCache,
          knownHydratedProjectCodes: knownHydratedProjectCodes.current,
          tasksPool: tasksPool.current,
        });

        if (healedProjects) {
          useAppStore.getState().setProjects(healedProjects);
        }

        const finalIds = new Set(finalTasks.map((t) => t.id));
        const changed = Array.from(pendingTasksChanged.current.values()).filter((t) => finalIds.has(t.id));
        const removedIds = Array.from(pendingTasksRemoved.current.keys());
        pendingTasksChanged.current.clear();
        pendingTasksRemoved.current.clear();
        if (changed.length > 0) offloadPersistSnapshot("taka_tasks", changed);
        removedIds.forEach((id) => {
          deleteLocalRecord("taka_tasks", id).catch(() => {});
        });

        const setNotifs = useAppStore.getState().setAllTasksForNotifs;
        if (setNotifs) setNotifs(finalTasks);
        pendingTasksMap.current = null;
      }
    }, 200);
  }, []);

  // Cleanup on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      tasksPool.current.forEach((u) => u());
      tasksPool.current.clear();
      tasksTTLTimeouts.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
      tasksTTLTimeouts.current.clear();
      if (tasksBatchTimer.current) clearTimeout(tasksBatchTimer.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // Lightweight Notification Query for Tasks (0 Read from Dexie with Safe Fallback)
  useEffect(() => {
    let isCancelled = false;
    let intervalId: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

    if (!querySignature || isTasksRequested) {
      return;
    }

    const fetchNotifs = async () => {
      if (isCancelled) return;
      try {
        const loadedNotifTasks = await loadNotificationTasks();
        if (isCancelled) return;
        setAllTasksForNotifs(loadedNotifTasks);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[useTasksHydration] Notification lightweight tasks query error:", e);
      }
    };

    fetchNotifs();

    const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 4 hours
    intervalId = setInterval(fetchNotifs, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => {
      isCancelled = true;
      if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);
    };
  }, [querySignature, isTasksRequested, setAllTasksForNotifs]);

  // Main Tasks Sync Logic
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!querySignature) return;

    const activeProjectIds = isTasksRequested
      ? [...new Set([...expandedProjectIds, ...searchActiveProjectIds])]
      : [];
    const currentSig = `${querySignature}_${activeProjectIds.join(",")}_${isTasksRequested}_${wakeRefreshCounter.current}`;

    if (prevQuerySigTasks.current !== querySignature) {
      if (prevQuerySigTasks.current !== "") {
        tasksPool.current.forEach((u) => u());
        tasksPool.current.clear();
        tasksTTLTimeouts.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
        tasksTTLTimeouts.current.clear();
        clearDeletedTasksTracker();
      }
      prevQuerySigTasks.current = querySignature;
    }
    prevSigTasks.current = currentSig;

    if (activeProjectIds.length === 0) {
      Array.from(tasksPool.current.keys()).forEach((key) => {
        if (!tasksTTLTimeouts.current.has(key)) {
          const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
            const unsub = tasksPool.current.get(key);
            if (unsub) unsub();
            tasksPool.current.delete(key);
            tasksTTLTimeouts.current.delete(key);
            usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${key}`);
          }, TTL_DURATION_MS);
          tasksTTLTimeouts.current.set(key, timeoutId);
        }
      });
      setIsTasksLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    let isCancelled = false;
    const runTasksSync = async () => {
      const currentProjects = useAppStore.getState().projects || [];
      const keysToHydrate = extractKeysToHydrate(activeProjectIds, currentProjects);

      await Promise.all([
        hydrateTasksForProjects(Array.from(keysToHydrate)),
        hydrateDelegationsForProjects(activeProjectIds),
      ]);
      if (isCancelled) return;
      const markers = await getLoadedMarkers();

      setIsTasksLoading(true);

      const projectsByYear = groupActiveProjectsByYear(activeProjectIds, currentProjects, globalYearFilter);

      const neededKeys = new Set<string>();
      let needTasksDeltaSync = false;

      projectsByYear.forEach((projs, year) => {
        if (projs.length > 10) {
          const yearKey = `year_${year}`;
          neededKeys.add(yearKey);

          if (tasksTTLTimeouts.current.has(yearKey)) {
            clearTimeout(tasksTTLTimeouts.current.get(yearKey)!);
            tasksTTLTimeouts.current.delete(yearKey);
          }

          projs.forEach((p) => {
            const pKey = `proj_${p.id}`;
            if (tasksPool.current.has(pKey)) {
              const u = tasksPool.current.get(pKey);
              if (u) u();
              tasksPool.current.delete(pKey);
              usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${pKey}`);
            }
          });

          if (!tasksPool.current.has(yearKey)) {
            const currentTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
            const hasLocalYearTasks = currentTasks.some(
              (t) => String(t.projectYear || "") === String(year)
            );
            const isArchivedPastYear = Number(year) < new Date().getFullYear() - 1;

            const deltaManaged =
              (hasLocalYearTasks && isArchivedPastYear) ||
              (DELTA_FIRST.tasks && hasLocalYearTasks && hasMarker(markers, "tasks_year", year));
            if (deltaManaged) {
              tasksPool.current.set(yearKey, () => {});
              setIsTasksLoading(false);
              needTasksDeltaSync = true;
              usePerformanceStore
                .getState()
                .trackGetDoc(
                  `taka_tasks_${yearKey}`,
                  true,
                  currentTasks.length,
                  "Dexie Local-First Tasks (0 Read full collection)"
                );
            } else {
              const colRef = collection(db, "artifacts", APP_ID, "public", "data", "taka_tasks");
              const qTasks = buildStoreAndYearQuery(
                colRef,
                "projectStore",
                "projectYear",
                year,
                userRole,
                allowedStores
              );

              usePerformanceStore.getState().registerListener(`taka_tasks_${yearKey}`);
              const unsub = onSnapshot(
                qTasks,
                (snapshot) => {
                  usePerformanceStore.getState().trackSnapshot(`taka_tasks_${yearKey}`, snapshot);
                  setIsTasksLoading(false);
                  const fromCache = snapshot.metadata.fromCache;
                  let changesToApply: any[] = snapshot.docChanges();
                  if (!fromCache && !snapshot.metadata.hasPendingWrites) {
                    const currentTasksNow = pendingTasksMap.current
                      ? Array.from(pendingTasksMap.current.values())
                      : useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
                    const zombieRemovals = buildZombieTaskRemovalsForYear(
                      snapshot.docs,
                      currentTasksNow,
                      year,
                      userRole,
                      allowedStores
                    );
                    if (zombieRemovals.length > 0) {
                      changesToApply = [...changesToApply, ...zombieRemovals];
                    }
                  }
                  batchTasksUpdate(changesToApply, fromCache);
                  setCloudStatus("online");

                  if (DELTA_FIRST.tasks && !fromCache) {
                    markLoaded("tasks_year", year).catch(() => {});
                    const u = tasksPool.current.get(yearKey);
                    tasksPool.current.set(yearKey, () => {});
                    u?.();
                    usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${yearKey}`);
                  }
                },
                (e) => {
                  setIsTasksLoading(false);
                  console.error("Tasks consolidated listener error:", e);
                  tasksPool.current.delete(yearKey);
                  usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${yearKey}`);
                  setCloudStatus("offline");
                }
              );
              tasksPool.current.set(yearKey, unsub);
            }
          }
        } else {
          const visibleSet = new Set((visibleActiveProjectIdsRef.current || []).map((id) => String(id)));
          const sortedProjs = [...projs].sort((a, b) => {
            const aVis = visibleSet.has(a.id) || visibleSet.has(a.code) ? 1 : 0;
            const bVis = visibleSet.has(b.id) || visibleSet.has(b.code) ? 1 : 0;
            return bVis - aVis;
          });

          sortedProjs.forEach((p) => {
            const projKey = `proj_${p.id}`;
            const yearKey = `year_${year}`;

            if (tasksPool.current.has(yearKey)) {
              neededKeys.add(yearKey);
              return;
            }

            neededKeys.add(projKey);

            if (tasksTTLTimeouts.current.has(projKey)) {
              clearTimeout(tasksTTLTimeouts.current.get(projKey)!);
              tasksTTLTimeouts.current.delete(projKey);
            }

            if (!tasksPool.current.has(projKey)) {
              const currentTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
              const localProjTasks = currentTasks.filter((t) => doesTaskBelongToProject(t, p));
              const hasLocalProjTasks =
                localProjTasks.length > 0 ||
                knownHydratedProjectCodes.current.has(String(p.id));
              const isArchivedPastYear = Number(year) < new Date().getFullYear() - 1;
              const isZeroTaskProject =
                (p.taskCount || 0) === 0 &&
                Array.isArray(p.taskSummaries) &&
                p.taskSummaries.length === 0 &&
                localProjTasks.length === 0;

              const deltaManaged =
                isZeroTaskProject ||
                (hasLocalProjTasks && isArchivedPastYear) ||
                (DELTA_FIRST.tasks &&
                  hasLocalProjTasks &&
                  (hasMarker(markers, "tasks_proj", p.id) ||
                    hasMarker(markers, "tasks_proj", p.code) ||
                    hasMarker(markers, "tasks_year", year)));
              if (deltaManaged) {
                tasksPool.current.set(projKey, () => {});
                setIsTasksLoading(false);
                if (!isZeroTaskProject) needTasksDeltaSync = true;
                usePerformanceStore
                  .getState()
                  .trackGetDoc(
                    `taka_tasks_${projKey}`,
                    true,
                    localProjTasks.length,
                    "Dexie Local-First Project Tasks (0 Read)"
                  );
              } else {
                const codeList = [p.code];
                if (p.linkCodes) {
                  const extra = String(p.linkCodes)
                    .split(/[,;/]+/)
                    .map((s) => s.trim())
                    .filter(Boolean);
                  extra.forEach((c) => {
                    if (!codeList.includes(c)) codeList.push(c);
                  });
                }
                const qTasks =
                  codeList.length > 1
                    ? query(
                        collection(db, "artifacts", APP_ID, "public", "data", "taka_tasks"),
                        where("projectCode", "in", codeList.slice(0, 10))
                      )
                    : query(
                        collection(db, "artifacts", APP_ID, "public", "data", "taka_tasks"),
                        where("projectCode", "==", p.code)
                      );

                usePerformanceStore.getState().registerListener(`taka_tasks_${projKey}`);
                const unsub = onSnapshot(
                  qTasks,
                  (snapshot) => {
                    knownHydratedProjectCodes.current.add(String(p.id));
                    usePerformanceStore.getState().trackSnapshot(`taka_tasks_${projKey}`, snapshot);
                    setIsTasksLoading(false);
                    const fromCache = snapshot.metadata.fromCache;

                    let compatibleChanges = filterCompatibleProjectChanges(
                      snapshot.docChanges(),
                      p
                    );

                    if (!fromCache && !snapshot.metadata.hasPendingWrites) {
                      const currentTasksNow = pendingTasksMap.current
                        ? Array.from(pendingTasksMap.current.values())
                        : useAppStore.getState().tasks || [];
                      const compatibleServerDocs = snapshot.docs.filter((docSnap) => {
                        const dt: any = docSnap.data();
                        if (dt?.isDeleted) return false;
                        if (dt && dt.projectId && p.id && String(dt.projectId).trim() !== String(p.id).trim()) return false;
                        if (
                          dt &&
                          dt.projectStore &&
                          p.store &&
                          String(dt.projectStore).trim().toLowerCase() !== String(p.store).trim().toLowerCase()
                        ) {
                          return false;
                        }
                        const dy = dt?.projectYear;
                        if (
                          dy !== undefined &&
                          dy !== null &&
                          dy !== "" &&
                          p.year !== undefined &&
                          p.year !== null &&
                          String(dy).trim() !== String(p.year).trim()
                        ) {
                          return false;
                        }
                        return true;
                      });
                      const zombieRemovals = buildZombieTaskRemovalsForProject(
                        compatibleServerDocs,
                        currentTasksNow,
                        p
                      );
                      if (zombieRemovals.length > 0) {
                        compatibleChanges = [...compatibleChanges, ...zombieRemovals];
                      }
                    }

                    batchTasksUpdate(compatibleChanges, fromCache);
                    setCloudStatus("online");

                    if (DELTA_FIRST.tasks && !fromCache) {
                      markLoaded("tasks_proj", p.id).catch(() => {});
                      const u = tasksPool.current.get(projKey);
                      tasksPool.current.set(projKey, () => {});
                      u?.();
                      usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${projKey}`);
                    }
                  },
                  (e) => {
                    setIsTasksLoading(false);
                    console.error("Tasks listener error:", e);
                    tasksPool.current.delete(projKey);
                    usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${projKey}`);
                    setCloudStatus("offline");
                  }
                );
                tasksPool.current.set(projKey, unsub);
              }
            }
          });
        }
      });

      if (needTasksDeltaSync) {
        pullAndApplyDeltas(undefined, true)
          .then(() => setCloudStatus("online"))
          .catch(() => {});
      }

      Array.from(tasksPool.current.keys()).forEach((key) => {
        if (!neededKeys.has(key)) {
          if (!tasksTTLTimeouts.current.has(key)) {
            const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
              const unsub = tasksPool.current.get(key);
              if (unsub) unsub();
              tasksPool.current.delete(key);
              tasksTTLTimeouts.current.delete(key);
              usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${key}`);
            }, TTL_DURATION_MS);
            tasksTTLTimeouts.current.set(key, timeoutId);
          }
        }
      });
    };

    runTasksSync().catch((err) => {
      console.warn("[useTasksHydration] runTasksSync error:", err);
    });

    const handleSleepWakeRecovery = () => {
      if (useAppStore.getState().isTasksRequested) {
        hydrateTasksFromLocal(useAppStore.getState().globalYearFilter).catch(() => {});
      }
      if (tasksPool.current.size > 0) {
        tasksPool.current.forEach((u, key) => {
          u();
          usePerformanceStore.getState().unregisterListener(`taka_tasks_${key}`);
        });
        tasksPool.current.clear();
        wakeRefreshCounter.current += 1;
        runTasksSync().catch(() => {});
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("taka_sleep_wake_recovery", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
    window.addEventListener("taka_network_recovered", handleSleepWakeRecovery);

    return () => {
      isCancelled = true;
      window.removeEventListener("taka_sleep_wake_recovery", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
      window.removeEventListener("taka_network_recovered", handleSleepWakeRecovery);
    };
  }, [
    querySignature,
    userRole,
    allowedStores,
    isTasksRequested,
    expandedProjectIds,
    searchActiveProjectIds,
    globalYearFilter,
    batchTasksUpdate,
    setIsTasksLoading,
    setCloudStatus,
  ]);
}

================ END FILE: src/hooks/useFirebaseDataParts/useTasksHydration.ts ================

================ FILE: src/hooks/useFirebaseDataParts/tasksHydrationHelpers.ts ================
import { Task, Project, DelegationGroup } from "../../types";
import { isTaskBelongsToProject, matchProjectCode } from "../../lib/utils";
import {
  getAppBootAgeMs,
  BOOT_SYNC_GUARD_MS,
  calculateProjectComputedDates,
  syncProjectDatesToFirestore,
} from "../../lib/projectDatesService";

export const tsOf = (v: any): number => {
  if (!v) return 0;
  if (typeof v === "string") {
    const n = Date.parse(v);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }
  if (typeof v?.toMillis === "function") {
    const n = v.toMillis();
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }
  if (typeof v?.seconds === "number") {
    return v.seconds * 1000 + Math.floor((v.nanoseconds || 0) / 1e6);
  }
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
};

/**
 * Checks if incoming doc from Firestore/cache is staler than existing local doc in memory.
 */
export function isStaleCacheDoc(existingDoc: Task | undefined, newDoc: Task): boolean {
  if (!existingDoc) return false;
  const existingTime = tsOf(existingDoc.updatedAt);
  const newTime = tsOf(newDoc?.updatedAt);
  if (existingTime <= 0 || newTime <= 0) return false;
  return existingTime > newTime;
}

/**
 * Extracts set of project keys/codes to hydrate from active project IDs.
 */
export function extractKeysToHydrate(
  activeProjectIds: string[],
  projects: Project[],
  globalYearFilter?: string
): Set<string> {
  const keys = new Set<string>();
  if (!Array.isArray(activeProjectIds) || activeProjectIds.length === 0) return keys;

  const idSet = new Set(activeProjectIds.map(id => String(id)));

  (projects || []).forEach((p) => {
    if (!p) return;
    const pId = String(p.id || '');
    const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim();
    const matchesById = pId && idSet.has(pId);
    const matchesByCode = pCode && idSet.has(pCode) && (
      !globalYearFilter ||
      globalYearFilter === 'all' ||
      !p.year ||
      String(p.year).trim() === String(globalYearFilter).trim()
    );
    if (matchesById || matchesByCode) {
      if (pCode) keys.add(pCode);
      if (p.linkCodes) {
        p.linkCodes.split(/[,;/]+/).forEach(c => {
          const trimmed = c.trim();
          if (trimmed) keys.add(trimmed);
        });
      }
    }
  });

  // Also include raw IDs if no project matched
  activeProjectIds.forEach(id => keys.add(String(id)));

  return keys;
}

/**
 * Groups active projects by year string.
 */
export function groupActiveProjectsByYear(
  activeProjectIds: string[],
  projects: Project[],
  globalYearFilter?: string
): Map<string, Project[]> {
  const map = new Map<string, Project[]>();
  if (!Array.isArray(projects)) return map;

  const activeIdSet = new Set(activeProjectIds.map(id => String(id)));

  projects.forEach((p) => {
    if (!p) return;
    const pId = String(p.id || '');
    const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim();
    const matchesById = pId && activeIdSet.has(pId);
    const matchesByCode = pCode && activeIdSet.has(pCode) && (
      !globalYearFilter ||
      globalYearFilter === 'all' ||
      !p.year ||
      String(p.year).trim() === String(globalYearFilter).trim()
    );

    if (matchesById || matchesByCode) {
      const year = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : (globalYearFilter || '2026');
      const list = map.get(year) || [];
      list.push(p);
      map.set(year, list);
    }
  });

  return map;
}

/**
 * Checks if a task belongs to a specific project by ID or (code + store + year).
 */
export function doesTaskBelongToProject(t: Task, p: Project): boolean {
  return isTaskBelongsToProject(t, p);
}

/**
 * Filters snapshot.docChanges() from a projectCode query so only tasks belonging to project `p` are applied.
 */
export function filterCompatibleProjectChanges(docChanges: any[], p: Project): any[] {
  return docChanges.filter((ch) => {
    const dt: any = ch.doc.data();
    if (dt && dt.projectId && p.id && String(dt.projectId).trim() !== String(p.id).trim()) return false;
    if (
      dt &&
      dt.projectStore &&
      p.store &&
      String(dt.projectStore).trim().toLowerCase() !== String(p.store).trim().toLowerCase()
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    const dy = dt?.projectYear;
    if (
      dy !== undefined &&
      dy !== null &&
      dy !== "" &&
      p.year !== undefined &&
      p.year !== null &&
      String(dy).trim() !== String(p.year).trim()
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}

/**
 * Builds synthetic "removed" docChanges for any local task in `year` that no longer exists in a server snapshot.
 */
export function buildZombieTaskRemovalsForYear(
  snapshotDocs: any[],
  currentTasks: Task[],
  year: string | number,
  userRole: string | undefined,
  allowedStores: string[]
): any[] {
  const serverIds = new Set<string>();
  snapshotDocs.forEach((d) => serverIds.add(d.id));
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  const allowedLower = allowedStores.map((s) => s.toLowerCase());
  const isStoreRestricted = userRole !== "admin" && allowedLower.length > 0 && !allowedLower.includes("all");
  const zombieRemovals: any[] = [];

  currentTasks.forEach((t) => {
    if (
      t?.id &&
      !t.id.startsWith("ts_") &&
      String(t.projectYear ?? "").trim() === String(year).trim() &&
      !serverIds.has(t.id)
    ) {
      if (isStoreRestricted && t.projectStore && !allowedLower.includes(String(t.projectStore).trim().toLowerCase())) {
        return;
      }
      const updatedMs = tsOf(t.updatedAt);
      if (!updatedMs || nowMs - updatedMs > 30000) {
        zombieRemovals.push({
          type: "removed",
          doc: { id: t.id, data: () => ({ ...t, isDeleted: true }) },
        });
      }
    }
  });

  return zombieRemovals;
}

/**
 * Builds synthetic "removed" docChanges for any local task of project `p` that no longer exists in a server snapshot.
 */
export function buildZombieTaskRemovalsForProject(
  snapshotDocs: any[],
  currentTasks: Task[],
  p: Project
): any[] {
  const serverIds = new Set<string>();
  snapshotDocs.forEach((d) => serverIds.add(d.id));
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  const zombieRemovals: any[] = [];

  currentTasks.forEach((t) => {
    if (t?.id && !t.id.startsWith("ts_") && doesTaskBelongToProject(t, p) && !serverIds.has(t.id)) {
      const updatedMs = tsOf(t.updatedAt);
      if (!updatedMs || nowMs - updatedMs > 30000) {
        zombieRemovals.push({
          type: "removed",
          doc: { id: t.id, data: () => ({ ...t, isDeleted: true }) },
        });
      }
    }
  });

  return zombieRemovals;
}

/**
 * Reconciles and heals project summaries and taskCount after a batch of task changes/removals.
 */
export function healProjectsAfterTasksBatch(params: {
  currentProjects: Project[];
  finalTasks: Task[];
  delegationGroups: DelegationGroup[];
  changedTasks: Map<string, Task>;
  removedTasks: Map<string, Task>;
  fromCache: boolean;
  knownHydratedProjectCodes: Set<string>;
  tasksPool: Map<string, () => void>;
}): Project[] | null {
  const {
    currentProjects,
    finalTasks,
    delegationGroups,
    changedTasks,
    removedTasks,
    fromCache,
    knownHydratedProjectCodes,
    tasksPool,
  } = params;

  if (!currentProjects || currentProjects.length === 0) return null;

  const touchedIds = new Set<string>();
  const touchedCodes = new Set<string>();
  const removedIdsLower = new Set<string>();

  const noteTaskTouch = (t: Partial<Task> | undefined) => {
    if (!t) return;
    if (t.projectId) touchedIds.add(String(t.projectId));
    if (t.projectCode) touchedCodes.add(String(t.projectCode).trim().toLowerCase());
  };

  changedTasks.forEach((t) => noteTaskTouch(t));
  removedTasks.forEach((t, id) => {
    removedIdsLower.add(String(id).trim().toLowerCase());
    noteTaskTouch(t);
  });

  if (touchedIds.size === 0 && touchedCodes.size === 0 && removedIdsLower.size === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  let projectsNeedUpdate = false;
  const canRunServerSelfHeal = !fromCache && (getAppBootAgeMs() >= BOOT_SYNC_GUARD_MS || knownHydratedProjectCodes.size > 0);

  const healedProjects = currentProjects.map((p) => {
    let workingProj = p;
    let summaryRemoved = false;

    // 1. Immediately purge any explicitly removed task IDs from p.taskSummaries (Rule 16)
    if (removedIdsLower.size > 0 && Array.isArray(workingProj.taskSummaries) && workingProj.taskSummaries.length > 0) {
      const filteredSummaries = workingProj.taskSummaries.filter((s: any) => {
        const sId = s?.id ? String(s.id).trim().toLowerCase() : "";
        return !sId || !removedIdsLower.has(sId);
      });
      if (filteredSummaries.length !== workingProj.taskSummaries.length) {
        summaryRemoved = true;
        projectsNeedUpdate = true;
        workingProj = {
          ...workingProj,
          taskSummaries: filteredSummaries,
          taskCount: filteredSummaries.length,
        };
      }
    }

    if (!canRunServerSelfHeal) return workingProj;

    const rawCodeCombined = workingProj.linkCodes ? `${workingProj.code || ''}, ${workingProj.linkCodes}` : String(workingProj.code || '');
    const pCodeParts = rawCodeCombined.toLowerCase().split(/[,;/]+/).map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const isProjectTouched =
      summaryRemoved ||
      touchedIds.has(String(workingProj.id)) ||
      pCodeParts.some((c) => touchedCodes.has(c));

    if (!isProjectTouched) return workingProj;
    if (getAppBootAgeMs() < BOOT_SYNC_GUARD_MS && !knownHydratedProjectCodes.has(String(workingProj.id))) return workingProj;

    const pTasks = finalTasks.filter((t) => !t.isDeleted && isTaskBelongsToProject(t, workingProj));
    const isFullyHydratedProject =
      knownHydratedProjectCodes.has(String(workingProj.id)) ||
      tasksPool.has(`proj_${workingProj.id}`) ||
      tasksPool.has(`year_${workingProj.year}`);

    if (!isFullyHydratedProject) {
      const existingMaxCount = Math.max(workingProj.taskCount || 0, Array.isArray(workingProj.taskSummaries) ? workingProj.taskSummaries.length : 0);
      if (pTasks.length === 0 || pTasks.length < existingMaxCount) return workingProj;
    }

    const baseProjForCalc = isFullyHydratedProject && pTasks.length === 0
      ? { ...workingProj, taskSummaries: [], taskCount: 0 }
      : workingProj;

    const computed = calculateProjectComputedDates(baseProjForCalc, pTasks, delegationGroups);
    const hasDiscrepancy =
      workingProj.taskCount !== computed.taskCount ||
      !workingProj.taskSummaries ||
      workingProj.taskSummaries.length !== (computed.taskSummaries?.length || 0) ||
      (workingProj.computedTakStart || '') !== (computed.computedTakStart || '') ||
      (workingProj.computedTakCompl || '') !== (computed.computedTakCompl || '') ||
      (workingProj.computedPlanProgress ?? 0) !== (computed.computedPlanProgress ?? 0) ||
      (workingProj.computedActProgress ?? 0) !== (computed.computedActProgress ?? 0);

    if (!hasDiscrepancy) return workingProj;

    projectsNeedUpdate = true;
    syncProjectDatesToFirestore(workingProj.id, computed).catch(() => {});

    return {
      ...workingProj,
      ...computed,
    };
  });

  return projectsNeedUpdate ? healedProjects : null;
}
================ END FILE: src/hooks/useFirebaseDataParts/tasksHydrationHelpers.ts ================
